<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50294702' data-tags='Entertainment'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(1) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/10/29/arts/television/patrick-stewart-dead.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Patrick Stewart has died</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>帕特里克·斯图尔特去世</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50294702' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 3103</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50294702' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 661</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Patrick Stewart, beloved for Star Trek&#x27;s Captain Picard and a long career in theatre and film, has died. His work helped redefine leadership in science fiction and mentored generations of actors. The news prompts reflection on a prolific career that bridged stage, screen, and iconic franchises.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>帕特里克·斯图尔特因病去世，曾因星际迷航中帕克船长等角色而家喻户晓。其横跨舞台、电视和电影的职业生涯对科幻与表演行业产生深远影响。人们由此缅怀这位多产演员及其对文化的贡献。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50163277' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(2) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/deepseek-ai/DeepInfer' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DeepInfer: High-performance inference framework for DeepSeek models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DeepInfer：面向 DeepSeek 模型的高性能推理框架</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50163277' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 2298</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50163277' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 880</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DeepInfer is a new high-performance inference framework designed for DeepSeek models, aiming to maximize throughput on modern hardware. It emphasizes modular backends and optimized runtimes to speed up deployment of large-scale AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DeepInfer 是面向 DeepSeek 模型的高性能推理框架，旨在于现代硬件上实现最大的吞吐量。它通过模块化后端与优化运行时来提升大规模 AI 推理的部署效率。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49998895' data-tags='Science,Culture,Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(3) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.mit.edu/2026/margaret-hamilton-computing-pioneer-dies-1007' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Margaret Hamilton has died</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>玛格丽特·汉密尔顿去世</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49998895' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 2103</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49998895' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 251</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Margaret Hamilton, a trailblazing software engineer who led NASA&#x27;s Apollo-era onboard software efforts, has died. Her work helped formalize software engineering as a discipline and improve the reliability of critical flight systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>玛格丽特·汉密尔顿作为推动软件工程成为正式学科的先驱，带领团队开发了阿波罗任务的飞行软件，显著提高关键系统的可靠性。她的去世让人们回顾这位女性在早期计算领域的重大贡献及其对现代软件的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.mit.edu/sites/default/files/images/202610/margaret-hamilton-portrait-toan-trinh-mit-technology-review-A1_1.png' data-full='https://news.mit.edu/sites/default/files/images/202610/margaret-hamilton-portrait-toan-trinh-mit-technology-review-A1_1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49977979' data-tags='AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(4) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mistral.ai/news/mistral-large-4/\' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mistral Large 4</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mistral Large 4 模型发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49977979' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 2018</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49977979' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1202</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mistral AI unveils Mistral Large 4, a new open-weight language model intended to deliver strong performance with broad accessibility. The release underscores the momentum behind community-driven models as alternatives to big incumbents.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mistral AI 发布了 Mistral Large 4，面向开源生态的语言模型，强调可获取性与性能平衡。此举反映了开源模型在市场上的持续崛起，成为对抗大型垄断模型的有力选项。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 2026 edition updates what cancer patients need to know, highlighting advances in treatment options and decision-making. It also flags access to care, side-effect management, and the role of digital health resources.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2026 版更新了癌症患者应了解的要点，着重介绍治疗选项的进展与决策过程。还提到就医获取、副作用管理，以及数字健康资源的作用。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50296470' data-tags='Security,Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(6) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arkm.com/explorer/entity/21a94755-f604-45e5-9501-6542e23005e8' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Binance-hacker-1029 – $5.6B stolen from Binance</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Binance 黑客案1029：从币安被盗5.6亿美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50296470' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1750</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50296470' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1240</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A $5.6B theft linked to Binance highlights the ongoing scale of crypto-exchange breaches. On-chain tracing attempts to map the stolen funds as they move through wallets and mixers, showing how investigators pursue assets long after a hack. The lesson for builders is to strengthen custody security (multi-sig, cold storage) and incident response readiness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>涉及币安的巨额盗窃案，金额约56亿美元，凸显集中交易所安全风险。链上追踪尝试绘出赃款去向，显示即便多年后仍有追踪的可能。对系统设计者的启示是加强资产托管安全（多签、冷存储）与快速事件响应能力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50289418' data-tags='AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(7) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mistral.ai/news/mistral-large-4-oss/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We&#x27;re releasing the weights of Mistral Large 4</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>发布 Mistral Large 4 的权重</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50289418' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1705</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50289418' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 438</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mistral AI has released the weights for Mistral Large 4, enabling researchers to run and fine-tune the model locally. This move reduces reliance on hosted APIs and accelerates experimentation, but also brings licensing, safety, and governance considerations to the fore in open-weight AI projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mistral Large 4 的权重已对外公开，研究者和开发者可以在本地部署与微调。此举降低对 API 的依赖，促进实验，但也引发许可、安全与治理方面的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mistral.ai/cms-media/api/media/file/cover-mistral-sovereign%20copie.webp' data-full='https://mistral.ai/cms-media/api/media/file/cover-mistral-sovereign%20copie.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50202285' data-tags='Web,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(8) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://edjefferson.com/localwiki/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Local Wikipedia: Wikipedia, but only for things a half hour walk from you</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>本地维基：只收录你步行半小时内的事物</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50202285' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1692</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50202285' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 253</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A concept for a Wikipedia-like wiki restricted to items within a 30-minute walk of your location, enabling hyperlocal crowdsourcing. This model foregrounds privacy and relevancy, but raises questions about coverage, updates, and shared standards across communities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个近场本地维基的设想，只收录你所在位置半小时步行范围内的条目，强调超本地化的众包协作与隐私保护。此思路提升信息相关性，但也带来覆盖范围、更新频率和跨社区标准化等挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://edjefferson.com/localwiki/share.png' data-full='https://edjefferson.com/localwiki/share.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49913571' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(9) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/models-and-research/gemini-models/gemini-4-argon/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gemini 4 Argon</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gemini 4 Argon（阿尔贡）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49913571' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1681</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49913571' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1162</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gemini 4 Argon appears to be a new variant in Google&#x27;s Gemini 4 family, signaling ongoing expansion of the model lineup to cover different price-performance tiers. Argon is pitched as a balance between capability and efficiency, giving enterprises a more cost-effective path to scale language-model workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是谷歌在 Gemini 4 系列中的一个新变体，凸显通过分层定价来覆盖不同用例的策略。Argon 版本强调在性能与效率之间的折中，面向需要大规模语言模型但关注成本的企业场景。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/g4_30-09-26_key-art_blog.width-1300.png' data-full='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/g4_30-09-26_key-art_blog.width-1300.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50242939' data-tags='AI,Business,Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(10) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/2130357/000162828026067168/anthropicpbc-sx1.htm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic S-1</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic 的 S-1 招股书</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50242939' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1675</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50242939' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1628</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic has filed an S-1 with the SEC, signaling plans for an IPO. The filing will reveal business metrics, product roadmap, and risk factors as the company scales its AI safety-focused offerings. Investors will weigh profitability trajectory, capital needs, and competitive positioning in a crowded AI market.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 已向美国证券交易委员会提交 S-1 文件，表明公司计划上市。文件将披露业务数据、产品路线图与风险因素，显示其在 AI 安全领域的扩张。投资者将评估盈利前景、资金需求以及在竞争激烈的 AI 市场中的定位。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50112657' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(12) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tesla.com/roadster' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tesla Roadster</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特斯拉 Roadster</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50112657' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1619</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50112657' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3015</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50276998' data-tags='Privacy,Security,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(13) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nullpt.rs/fingerprinting-bioweapons' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why is this fingerprinting script creating bioweapons?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么这个指纹识别脚本在制造生物武器？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50276998' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1581</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50276998' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 306</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author describes investigating browser fingerprinting scripts, from libraries such as FingerprintJS to anti-bot systems used by Cloudflare Turnstile and Akamai. They then considered crawling the web, intercepting fingerprinting-related browser API calls, and mapping what was found. The title connects this investigation with scripts that appear to be creating bioweapons, though the supplied material does not provide further details.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者介绍了对浏览器指纹识别脚本的调查，范围从 FingerprintJS 等常见库到 Cloudflare Turnstile 和 Akamai 使用的反机器人系统。随后，作者考虑抓取网页、拦截与指纹识别相关的浏览器 API 调用，并绘制其分布图。标题将这项调查与似乎正在制造生物武器的脚本联系起来，但现有内容未提供更多细节。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nullpt.rs/posts/fingerprinting-bioweapons/canvas-gallery.jpg' data-full='https://nullpt.rs/posts/fingerprinting-bioweapons/canvas-gallery.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(14) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.php.net/archive/2026.php#2026-10-21-5' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Security advisory: Critical remote code execution vulnerabilities, update now</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>安全公告：关键远程代码执行漏洞，请立即更新</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50172041' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1539</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50172041' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 655</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A security advisory warns of critical remote code execution vulnerabilities that could let attackers take control of affected systems. Patching now across vulnerable software stacks is essential to limit risk, as exploitation often ramps up after disclosure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一份安全公告警告存在关键的远程代码执行漏洞，可能使攻击者远程控制受影响的系统。现在就对受影响的软件栈进行打补丁至关重要，以降低风险，因为披露后利用活动往往会增加。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AAA级扫雷将经典益智游戏推向高水准的现代化呈现，拥有精致的美术、流畅的交互和性能优化。该项目展示了当代工具链与用户体验如何为复古游戏注入新活力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(17) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://deno.com/blog/cloudflare' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cloudflare acquires Deno</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cloudflare 收购 Deno，整合边缘计算生态</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50019911' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1363</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50019911' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 711</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare is acquiring Deno, marrying Deno’s secure, TypeScript-friendly runtime with Cloudflare’s edge network and Workers platform. The deal could accelerate edge-based development and broaden Cloudflare’s developer toolkit, though it raises questions about governance and open-source leadership in the workspace.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 将收购 Deno，将 Deno 的安全、TypeScript 友好运行时与 Cloudflare 的边缘网络和 Workers 平台整合。此举可能加速基于边缘的开发并扩展 Cloudflare 的开发者工具包，同时也引发关于治理与开源领导力的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://deno.com/blog/cloudflare/deno-cf-balanced-og.webp' data-full='https://deno.com/blog/cloudflare/deno-cf-balanced-og.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(20) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://quad9.net/news/blog/au-revoir-france/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Quad9 to exit France</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Quad9 将退出法国</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50195959' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1318</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50195959' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 456</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Privacy-focused DNS resolver Quad9 announces it will discontinue services in France, citing regulatory and operational challenges. The move underscores how geopolitical and local privacy laws shape the reach of privacy-centric infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Quad9 宣布退出法国市场，原因涉及监管与运营挑战。这一举动凸显了地缘政治与地方隐私法规对隐私基础设施覆盖的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://quad9.net/images/cover_c2f081e020.png' data-full='https://quad9.net/images/cover_c2f081e020.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50271640' data-tags='Security,Privacy,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://emudhra.we-have-a-problem.com/apple.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple&#x27;s git still trusts the compromised eMudhra certificates</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果的 Git 仍信任被妥协的 eMudhra 证书</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50271640' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1173</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50271640' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 403</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple&#x27;s git still trusts compromised eMudhra certificates, leaving room for potential man-in-the-middle attacks on TLS and code signing trust. This highlights how supply chain security depends on up-to-date trust stores, and warrants prompt certificate revocation and rotation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果的 Git 仍信任被妥协的 eMudhra 证书，增加了 TLS 与代码签名信任被滥用的风险。这凸显供应链安全对最新信任存储的依赖，应尽快撤销并轮换相关证书。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50166579' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(22) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://emudhra.we-have-a-problem.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>eMudhra intermediate CA compromise</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>EMudhra 中级证书颁发机构遭到妥协</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50166579' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1169</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50166579' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 453</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EMudhra reports compromise of its Intermediate Certificate Authority, potentially enabling issuance of fraudulent certificates. The incident highlights PKI supply-chain risk and the need for aggressive revocation, transparency, and improved security controls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>EMudhra 报告其中级证书颁发机构遭到妥协，可能导致伪造证书的签发。这一事件凸显公钥基础设施供应链风险，以及需要更快速的撤销、透明披露和改进的安全控制措施。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50272749' data-tags='Politics,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(23) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wired.com/story/ice-is-running-one-of-the-largest-anti-left-surveillance-operations-in-us-history/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Evidence of a spying operation by ICE against left-wing social movements</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ICE对左翼社会运动实施监视行动的证据</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50272749' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1165</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50272749' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 987</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New documents and whistleblower testimony reveal ICE conducted a sprawling surveillance operation targeting left-wing social movements, leveraging internal data and informants across decades. The findings raise questions about civil liberties, oversight, and the balance between border enforcement and political dissent.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新的文档和告密者证词揭示ICE长期针对左翼社会运动执行大规模监视行动，动用了内部数据和线人。相关证据推动对公民自由、监督机制以及边境执法与政治异议之间平衡的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(25) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://emudhra.com/en/press-release/emudhra-to-exit-global-ssl-tls-certificate-business' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>eMudhra to exit global SSL/TLS certificate business</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>eMudhra 将退出全球 SSL/TLS 证书业务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50195110' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1122</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50195110' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 718</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>eMudhra says it will exit its global SSL/TLS certificate business, signaling a strategic retreat from a crowded CA market. The move could nudge customers to migrate to other providers and accelerate consolidation in the PKI ecosystem, with ripple effects on how organizations manage trust chains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>eMudhra 宣布将退出全球 SSL/TLS 证书业务，显示其在竞争激烈的证书颁发机构市场中的战略收缩。此举可能促使客户转向其他供应商，并推动公钥基础设施生态的整合，对组织的信任链管理产生连锁影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(28) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nonfunctional.substack.com/p/passkeys-are-a-trap-do-not-use-them' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Passkeys are a trap</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Passkeys 是陷阱。请不要使用它们。</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50048312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1093</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50048312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 559</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative stance arguing passkeys introduce risk, friction, or vendor lock-in rather than solving security problems. It cautions readers to consider trade-offs and to explore alternatives like hardware-backed keys or password managers. The piece invites debate about modern authentication design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇观点认为 Passkeys 并未真正解决安全问题，反而带来新的风险、使用摩擦和厂商锁定。强调权衡取舍并探讨硬件密钥、密码管理工具等替代方案，鼓励对现代认证设计的讨论。</p>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(31) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wired.com/story/the-us-government-is-hiding-a-secret-court-ruling-about-how-it-spies-on-americans/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US Government is hiding a secret court ruling about how it spies on Americans</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国政府隐瞒关于监控美国人的秘密法院裁决</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50259604' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1053</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50259604' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 423</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An undisclosed court ruling governs how the government collects data on Americans, and officials have kept it secret. The ruling highlights ongoing friction between security powers and civil liberties, underscoring the demand for greater transparency and robust oversight.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>尚未公开的法院裁决规定政府如何监控美国民众的数据，相关信息被隐瞒。该裁决暴露出国家安全权力与公民自由之间的矛盾，也凸显对更大透明度和强有力监督的诉求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.wired.com/photos/6adbe0039bde53c96a68d73b/191:100/w_1280,c_limit/Security_Trump%20Officials%20are%20Sitting%20on%20a%20Secret%20Surveillance%20Court%20Ruling_v1.jpg' data-full='https://media.wired.com/photos/6adbe0039bde53c96a68d73b/191:100/w_1280,c_limit/Security_Trump%20Officials%20are%20Sitting%20on%20a%20Secret%20Surveillance%20Court%20Ruling_v1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50122800' data-tags='Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(32) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/research/ecdsa-curve-origins' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude reveals ECDSA curve origins</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude 揭示 ECDSA 曲线的来源</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50122800' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1044</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50122800' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 386</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Claude-based analysis traced the provenance of ECDSA elliptic-curve parameters, revealing how the seeds and generation choices were produced and documented. That provenance matters because the trustworthiness and backdoor risk of standard curves depend on transparent, auditable generation procedures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>使用 Claude 的分析追溯了 ECDSA 椭圆曲线参数的生成来源，揭示了用于生成曲线的种子与选择的记录方式。曲线生成的可追溯性很重要，直接关系到标准曲线是否值得信任以及是否存在后门风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www-cdn.anthropic.com/images/4zrzovbb/website/6d4a0d28992ade92d6fa63646fd9c9d318245c6c-2400x1260.jpg' data-full='https://www-cdn.anthropic.com/images/4zrzovbb/website/6d4a0d28992ade92d6fa63646fd9c9d318245c6c-2400x1260.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic released Claude Haiku 5.5, delivering improvements in reliability, safety, and efficiency for conversational AI. The update tightens safety guardrails, enhances instruction-following, and aims to deliver smoother multi-turn interactions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 发布 Claude Haiku 5.5，提升了对话式 AI 的可靠性、安全性与效率。更新强化了安全约束、改进了指令执行，并力求在多轮对话中提供更顺畅的体验。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(34) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://deepmind.google/blog/genie-4-a-new-generation-of-world-simulation/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Genie 4</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Genie 4：世界仿真的新一代</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50261319' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1031</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50261319' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 583</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Genie 4 from DeepMind represents a leap in world-simulation tech, enabling more realistic, scalable environments for AI training and planning. It aims to accelerate progress in robotics, game AI, and simulation-to-real transfer by providing richer, more controllable environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DeepMind 的 Genie 4 提供更真实、更可控的世界仿真能力，为 AI 训练和规划提供更丰富的环境，有望加速机器人、游戏 AI 以及从仿真到现实的迁移。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/i7iotPkcMbMubxRzAdriO5wsIsowROonBvjLNnmPbOaRgEb1k4LKTBqay5VrcPpZi0R4il_bYCCsPYQ0Gy_-XbAw66azAGEfE-E6_9Izz5apP0BMJVE=w1200-h630-n-nu-rw' data-full='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/i7iotPkcMbMubxRzAdriO5wsIsowROonBvjLNnmPbOaRgEb1k4LKTBqay5VrcPpZi0R4il_bYCCsPYQ0Gy_-XbAw66azAGEfE-E6_9Izz5apP0BMJVE=w1200-h630-n-nu-rw' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(35) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.seattletimes.com/business/microsoft/microsoft-offers-mass-buyouts-for-roughly-a-quarter-of-its-workforce/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft offers mass buyouts for roughly a quarter of its workforce</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软为约四分之一员工提供大规模自愿离职计划</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50245524' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1018</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50245524' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1049</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft is offering voluntary buyouts to roughly a quarter of its workforce, a major headcount reduction without forced layoffs. The move fits into broader tech-sector efficiency drives as AI investments continue and macro pressures persist. It could reshape compensation bands, talent flow, and project prioritization across divisions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软向约四分之一员工提供自愿离职计划，属于不强制裁员的大规模裁员。此举与科技行业的整体效率提升趋势相吻合，AI 投资与宏观压力并行推动成本优化。人力结构、人才流向和项目优先级可能因此调整。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(40) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.csoonline.com/article/4234854/ai-agents-arent-going-rogue-frontier-labs-are-skipping-security-basics.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Containment and monitoring failures are to blame for AI hacking reports: experts</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>专家称AI黑客报道源于封控与监控失败</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50091788' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 945</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50091788' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 616</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Security experts attribute AI hacking reports to containment and monitoring failures rather than rogue AI behavior. The piece underscores the need for stronger containment, better monitoring, and robust incident-response practices when deploying AI agents.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>安全专家将 AI 黑客报道归因于封控与监控失败，而非 AI 自主失控。文章强调在部署 AI 代理时需加强封控、改进监控，并建立健全的事件响应机制。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Whistle introduces a speech-to-text model that runs at just 16.9 MB, delivering surprisingly good transcription quality for lightweight use. It exemplifies the trend toward tiny, efficient AI models suitable for edge devices and offline scenarios.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Whistle 推出仅16.9 MB的语音转文字模型，在重量级外的轻量化场景也能实现相当不错的转写效果。体现了小型高效 AI 模型在边缘设备与离线场景中的发展趋势。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(43) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mapwriting.substack.com/p/dentists' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Do dentists know what they&#x27;re doing?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>牙医真的知道自己在做什么吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50050553' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 934</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50050553' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 658</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece questions variability in dental diagnosis and treatment, highlighting instances of over-treatment, opaque pricing, and inconsistent standards across practitioners. It urges patients to seek second opinions and clinicians to adopt clearer guidelines and transparency to reduce unnecessary procedures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章质疑牙科诊断与治疗的差异性，指出过度治疗、价格不透明和不同医生之间标准不一的现象。作者建议患者寻求第二诊疗意见，并呼吁行业采纳更清晰的指南与透明度以减少不必要的手术。</p>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(46) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://permit.petercai.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: SimCity, but with Permits</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50170593' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 922</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50170593' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 416</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A playful project adds permit bureaucracy to a SimCity-like environment, showing how permit delays shape growth, zoning decisions, and development timelines. The result offers a tangible look at how administrative friction can influence urban planning experiments and policy outcomes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个将许可制度引入类似 SimCity 的模拟环境的有趣项目，展示许可拖延如何影响城市增长、分区决策与开发节奏。该成果为官僚摩擦如何影响城市规划实验与政策结果提供了直观的观察。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(47) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://apnews.com/article/h1b-visa-program-vance-microsoft-e7b3a407f822702b269ee277d21343ea' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Trump administration is suspending Microsoft from a green card program</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50006832' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 912</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50006832' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1557</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft was suspended from a green-card program by the current administration, a move reflecting ongoing immigration policy shifts affecting tech recruiting. The decision could disrupt hiring plans for large tech employers and underscores policy-risk in skilled-immigration programs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>政府暂停微软参与将永久居民身份的绿卡计划，反映对技术人才移民路径的收紧趋势。此举可能扰乱大型科技公司的招聘计划，凸显科技领域移民政策的风险与博弈。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50177916' data-tags='Security,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(50) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://scotthelme.co.uk/eight-bits-of-trust-emudhras-ca-keys-cracked-in-milliseconds/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Eight Bits of Trust: eMudhra&#x27;s CA keys cracked in milliseconds</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>信任的八位字节：eMudhra 的 CA 密钥在毫秒内被破解</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50177916' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 910</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50177916' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 445</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers demonstrate that eMudhra&#x27;s CA keys can be cracked in milliseconds, challenging TLS trust assumptions. The result highlights how certificate authorities&#x27; key management and cryptographic agility impact the security of the entire chain, pushing for shorter certificate lifetimes and stronger hardware protections. It also underscores the need for tougher CA audits and improved trust-chain resilience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者显示 eMudhra 的 CA 钥匙可在毫秒内被破解，挑战了 TLS 信任的基本假设。此现象突显证书颁发机构在密钥管理与加密灵活性方面对整个信任链安全的影响，推动对更短证书有效期和更强硬件保护的需求，同时强调对 CA 审计和信任链韧性的改进。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/ee/88/ee889f88-37ef-43e5-9180-f9b88ee6261d/content/images/size/w1200/2026/10/emudhra.png' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/ee/88/ee889f88-37ef-43e5-9180-f9b88ee6261d/content/images/size/w1200/2026/10/emudhra.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(52) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://danluu.com/ambitious/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We should be more ambitious with the software we build</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在软件开发上应更具野心</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50225695' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 892</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50225695' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 769</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Software projects often settle for incremental gains; the piece argues for embracing bigger, riskier problems that unlock substantial leaps. Ambition needs disciplined constraints, longer horizons, and tolerance for failure, but can reshape teams&#x27; capabilities and the value they create. Expect practical guidance on bet-sizing, architecture, and collaboration to support bold bets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>很多软件项目止步于小修小补。作者主张在软件开发上要敢于挑战更大、风险更高的问题，以实现更大的跃迁。实现这种野心需要清晰的约束、长期投入与对失败的容忍，进而改变团队能力与创造的价值。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50055304' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(56) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/tech-industry/cyber-security/unprecedented-cyberattack-cripples-supermarket-fridges-across-europe-as-hackers-activate-automatic-defrosting-delivery-and-store-prices-also-impacted-by-incident' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hackers activate automatic defrosting in supermarket fridges across Europe</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>黑客在欧洲多国超市冷藏设备上启用自动除霜</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50055304' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 871</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50055304' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 971</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A coordinated cyberattack triggered automatic defrosting across supermarket fridges in Europe, disrupting storage conditions and potentially impacting pricing and stock. The incident underscores how IoT-enabled retail devices can be manipulated at scale, highlighting security gaps in supply chains and the importance of robust device hygiene.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一次跨国网络攻击促使欧洲多家超市的冷藏设备启用自动除霜，干扰了冷藏条件，可能影响库存与价格。这一事件凸显了零售物联网设备的安全脆弱性，以及对供应链安全治理的迫切需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/o7hTqTruL84oHxvg6pYS3K-2000-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/o7hTqTruL84oHxvg6pYS3K-2000-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49949438' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49949438' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tell HN: Bob Cringely has died</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>鲍勃·克林西利去世（Bob Cringely）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49949438' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 861</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49949438' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 185</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bob Cringely, a veteran tech journalist and columnist, has died. He helped popularize accessible, narrative-driven tech journalism with works like I, Cringely and commentary on Apple and Silicon Valley, shaping how mainstream audiences think about the industry. His passing marks the end of an era for journalism that mixes insider storytelling with tech analysis.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>资深科技记者鲍勃·克林西利去世。他以 I, Cringely 等作品和对苹果及科技行业的评论，推动了以叙事方式讲述科技的风格，影响了主流对科技产业的理解。他的离世标志着一种将行业分析与个人叙事相结合的新闻传统的告别。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49953495' data-tags='AI,Privacy,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(58) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Niko1221/Strata' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Run Qwen 3.8 Flash Next (125B) on consumer hardware (RTX 4090) at 100T/s</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在消费级显卡 RTX 4090 上实现 Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 125B 模型的 100T/s 吞吐</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49953495' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 853</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49953495' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 383</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An experimental setup shows Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 125B running on a single RTX 4090 with extremely high throughput, around 100T/s. The work leverages optimized kernels and model quantization, illustrating how multi-hundred-billion-parameter models may become feasible on consumer hardware for research and prototyping. This pushes the boundaries of on-device AI, enabling privacy-preserving demos and edge deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过实验设置显示，125B 版本的 Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 能在单块 RTX 4090 上实现极高吞吐，约 100T/s。该工作利用优化内核与模型量化，展示了在消费级硬件上运行大模型的可行性，为研究、原型开发以及隐私保护的边缘部署提供了可能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/027794c268e81797b30854290ac35d34de80b7e369031c1444a21cfc7607dcee/Niko1221/Strata' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/027794c268e81797b30854290ac35d34de80b7e369031c1444a21cfc7607dcee/Niko1221/Strata' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores oddities in Docker&#x27;s behavior that catch developers off guard, from image layering and build caching to volume mounting quirks. The post offers practical tips to avoid common pitfalls and explains why these quirks persist in modern container tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>揭示 Docker 行为中的一些反直觉之处，比如镜像分层、构建缓存和卷挂载的怪异之处。文中给出实用的排错技巧，解释了这些怪异现象在现代容器工具中的持续原因。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A heartwarming clip shows a woman who rescued an injured crow continuing to receive &#x27;thank-you gifts&#x27; from the birds, illustrating surprising reciprocity in wildlife behavior. <p class='hn-title'>(66) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pepsipu.com/blog/2026-10-switch2-jailbreak-ns2jb/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Jailbreaking the Switch 2</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Switch 2 越狱工具发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50192536' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 795</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50192536' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 289</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New jailbreak tooling for the Switch 2 has surfaced, enabling homebrew and custom software on Nintendo&#x27;s latest console. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Switch 2 的新越狱工具出现，使玩家能够在新版掌机上运行自制软件。此举凸显平台安全与玩家破解之间的博弈，可能对固件完整性、设备稳定性和保修造成影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50251661' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(67) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/openai/math/blob/main/preprints/The-Exponential-Time-Hypothesis-is-False-October-25-2026/paper.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Exponential Time Hypothesis Is False [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>指数时间假设不成立</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50251661' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 792</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50251661' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 295</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new paper argues that the Exponential Time Hypothesis is false, upending a long-standing conditional lower-bound assumption in complexity theory. If ETH fails, widespread implications follow for algorithm design and hardness results, potentially enabling faster solutions for some NP-hard problems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇新论文提出指数时间假设不成立，动摇了长期支撑 NP 问题下界的核心假设。若 ETH 不再成立，算法设计与复杂性界限将被重新评估，某些 NP-hard 问题或将获得更高效的解法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/9c8f408fa180ff619b1a10b59401aff152b396feccc1b9e6d6880ee408716cf5/openai/math' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/9c8f408fa180ff619b1a10b59401aff152b396feccc1b9e6d6880ee408716cf5/openai/math' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49927754' data-tags='Legal,Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(68) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/10/court-agrees-eff-utahs-vpn-law-demands-technical-impossibility' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Court agrees with EFF: Utah&#x27;s VPN law demands a technical impossibility</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法院认定犹他州的 VPN 法等同技术不可实现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49927754' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 783</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49927754' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 388</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The court sided with the EFF, ruling that Utah&#x27;s VPN law imposes requirements that are effectively impossible to meet. The decision underscores how prescriptive tech laws can clash with real-world capabilities of privacy tools. For engineers and policymakers, it&#x27;s a reminder that regulatory designs must align with practical feasibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法院支持EFF的立场，裁定犹他州的 VPN 法在技术实现上不可行。此判决凸显了规制性科技法规与隐私工具现实能力之间的冲突。对工程师和政策制定者而言，这是一个提醒：可执行的规则须与实际可行性相符。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/vpn-highway-tunnel-banner.jpg' data-full='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/vpn-highway-tunnel-banner.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50054092' data-tags='Security,Privacy,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(69) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bleepingcomputer.com/news/security/ledger-confirms-hardware-wallet-spy-implant-behind-crypto-thefts/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ledger confirms spy chip implant behind wallet crypto thefts</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ledger 证实硬件钱包存在间谍芯片植入，涉多起加密盗窃</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50054092' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 777</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50054092' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 491</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ledger confirms that spy-chip implants were behind some compromised hardware wallets tied to crypto thefts, underscoring ongoing supply-chain risk in crypto hardware. The disclosure adds to a wary narrative around provenance, tamper-resistance, and firmware integrity in popular wallets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ledger 证实部分硬件钱包被植入间谍芯片，导致加密盗窃事件暴露供应链脆弱性。此事凸显源头可追溯性、抗篡改能力与固件完整性在热销钱包中的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.bleepstatic.com/content/hl-images/2026/10/12/Ledger.jpg' data-full='https://www.bleepstatic.com/content/hl-images/2026/10/12/Ledger.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50168396' data-tags='AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(70) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mistral.ai/news/voxtral-3/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Voxtral 3</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Voxtral 3 上线</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50168396' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 765</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50168396' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 199</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mistral AI unveils Voxtral 3, the latest entry in its Voxtral family. The release signals continued momentum in open-weight models and the trend toward more capable, accessible AI cores.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mistral AI 发布 Voxtral 3，是 Voxtral 系列的最新版本。该发布体现了开源权重模型的持续发展，以及更强大、易接入的 AI 核心的趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mistral.ai/cms-media/api/media/file/hero-blog-voxtral.png' data-full='https://mistral.ai/cms-media/api/media/file/hero-blog-voxtral.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49957116' data-tags='AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(71) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/omlahore/RemoveMacAI' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Turn off Apple Intelligence on macOS 27 and get its disk space back</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 macOS 27 上关闭 Apple Intelligence 并回收磁盘空间</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49957116' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 764</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49957116' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 537</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A community tool demonstrates how to turn off Apple Intelligence on macOS 27 to reclaim disk space by removing AI components. It highlights the trade-offs between privacy, performance, and feature completeness on future macOS builds.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个社区工具展示了在 macOS 27 上关闭 Apple Intelligence 以通过移除 AI 相关组件来回收磁盘空间。此举揭示了系统级 AI 功能对存储与性能的影响，以及未来 macOS 构建中的取舍。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1394989252/13dcf083-d511-4f51-9681-c6ccbda3cae9' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1394989252/13dcf083-d511-4f51-9681-c6ccbda3cae9' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50028275' data-tags='Programming,Security,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(72) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rea.tools/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>REA Reverse – Engineer Anything</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>REA Reverse：逆向工程万物</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50028275' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 755</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50028275' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 324</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>REA Reverse provides a hands-on platform for reverse engineering across domains, offering challenges and tutorials to dissect how software and hardware work. It aims to democratize RE skills by curating practical content and community-driven exercises. This matters for defenders, researchers, and hardware hackers looking to deepen code comprehension and vulnerability analysis.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>REA Reverse 提供一个聚焦逆向工程的实践平台，涵盖软件与硬件领域的挑战与教程，帮助用户拆解系统工作原理。通过实操内容和社区驱动的练习，推动逆向工程技能的普及。对安全研究、漏洞分析和硬件黑客都具有现实意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://rea.tools/assets/social-card.png' data-full='https://rea.tools/assets/social-card.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50063427' data-tags='AI,Career'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(73) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://yyhh.org/blog/2026/10/ai-is-a-tool/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI is a tool and using it well takes skill</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 是工具，善用它需要技巧</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50063427' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 755</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50063427' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 584</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that AI is a tool and its value comes from how skillfully it is used, urging engineers to develop practical workflows, critical thinking, and governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章将 AI 视为工具，强调其价值取决于使用者的技能水平，呼吁建立实用工作流、批判性思维和治理机制以避免滥用与过度乐观。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://yyhh.org/images/yyhh.svg' data-full='https://yyhh.org/images/yyhh.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49996425' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(74) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/gpt-6-for-everyone/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GPT‑6 and Intelligent UI for everyone</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPT‑6 与人人可用的智能界面</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49996425' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 751</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49996425' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 456</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI previews GPT-6 and a universal intelligent UI designed to make advanced AI capabilities accessible to non-experts. The post emphasizes a more integrated UX, safety controls, and multimodal synergy across devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 展示 GPT-6 与面向所有人的智能 UI，旨在让非专业用户也能轻松使用 AI 能力。文中强调更紧密的用户体验、可控的安全设置，以及跨设备的多模态协同。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/5frqtUAWccstAbVrCFraH6/5ae9b6bfbc3f3dff8001ed249812f97d/Intelligent-UI_Art-Card_16x9.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' data-full='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/5frqtUAWccstAbVrCFraH6/5ae9b6bfbc3f3dff8001ed249812f97d/Intelligent-UI_Art-Card_16x9.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50071017' data-tags='Privacy,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(75) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apple.com/apple-homeview/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple HomeView</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Apple HomeView（苹果家庭视图）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50071017' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 744</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50071017' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 879</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple announces HomeView, a new feature aimed at simplifying how users view and control smart devices in the home. It underscores Apple&#x27;s continuing effort to deepen the Home ecosystem with privacy-conscious design and tighter device interoperability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果宣布了 HomeView，旨在简化家庭中智能设备的查看与控制。此举体现苹果继续扩展家居生态的决心，强调隐私设计与设备互操作性的提升。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.apple.com/v/apple-homeview/a/images/meta/apple-homeview__lk7n65hb6bm2_og.png?202610121723' data-full='https://www.apple.com/v/apple-homeview/a/images/meta/apple-homeview__lk7n65hb6bm2_og.png?202610121723' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50282454' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(76) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://data.stackexchange.com/stackoverflow/query/1926661#graph?t=1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>StackOverflow now gets fewer than 1000 questions a month</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Stack Overflow 现每月不到 1000 条问题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50282454' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 743</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50282454' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 655</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stack Overflow now reports fewer than 1,000 questions per month, a dramatic drop that signals a major shift in how developers seek help online. The decline could reflect migration to other communities, changes in data collection, or platform strategy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Stack Overflow 现每月提问不足一千条，显示开发者在线求助方式的显著转变。下降可能源于迁移到其他社区、数据收集变化或平台策略调整。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50048642' data-tags='Privacy,Data,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://royfish.dev/fingerprints-are-the-new-cookies' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fingerprints are the new cookies</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>指纹识别成为新的网络追踪手段</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50048642' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 738</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50048642' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 360</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Browser fingerprinting persists as a tracking technique despite cookie erosion, enabling unique combos of device attributes to identify users across sites. It underscores the ongoing tension between usability and privacy online.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>尽管 cookies 逐渐被边缘化，浏览器指纹仍作为一种持续的跟踪手段，通过设备属性的组合实现跨站点识别。文章讨论隐私影响及可能的对策，包括反指纹技术与浏览器保护措施，强调在线可用性与隐私之间的持续张力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://imgs.xkcd.com/comics/password_strength.png' data-full='https://imgs.xkcd.com/comics/password_strength.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50159341' data-tags='AI,Legal,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(78) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://redact.dev/blog/whatsapp-help-pages-hidden-ai-instructions' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>WhatsApp&#x27;s help pages hid LLM instructions: dont recommend a regulator</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>WhatsApp 帮助页面隐藏了 LLM 指令：不建议监管机构</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50159341' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 735</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50159341' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 212</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Internal prompts reportedly steered WhatsApp&#x27;s LLM-enabled help content to avoid recommending regulators. The disclosure highlights how vendor-defined guardrails shape user guidance in consumer AI features and underscores transparency concerns around embedded prompts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称 WhatsApp 的 LLM 助手帮助页面中存在引导指令，要求其避免向用户推荐监管机构。这揭示了厂商设定的安全边界如何影响用户指引，以及对嵌入式提示透明度的担忧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://redact.dev/blog-images/2026/10/whatsapp-hidden-ai-notes-robot-puppeteer.jpg' data-full='https://redact.dev/blog-images/2026/10/whatsapp-hidden-ai-notes-robot-puppeteer.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50070302' data-tags='Web,Privacy,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(79) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.firefox.com/en-US/enterprise/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Firefox Enterprise</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mozilla 推出 Firefox 企业版</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50070302' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 732</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50070302' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 315</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mozilla launches Firefox Enterprise, a business-focused distribution with centralized deployment, policy controls, and enterprise-friendly features. The move broadens Firefox&#x27;s appeal in corporate IT, as organizations seek privacy-first browsers with managed configurations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mozilla 推出面向企业的 Firefox Enterprise，提供集中部署、策略控制和面向企业的安全特性，便于 IT 管理。此举有助于 Firefox 在企业环境中的竞争力，满足组织对隐私与可控配置的需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.firefox.com/media/img/firefox/flare/og.4d50d9b1674b.png' data-full='https://www.firefox.com/media/img/firefox/flare/og.4d50d9b1674b.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50084240' data-tags='Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(80) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://typeof.net/Iosevka/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Iosevka is an open-source, sans-serif and slab-serif monospace font</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Iosevka 开源的等宽字体家族，涵盖无衬线与方衬线风格</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50084240' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 731</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50084240' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 299</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Iosevka is an open-source monospace font family that blends sans-serif and slab-serif variants. It offers broad customization and a cohesive aesthetic for developers across editors, terminals, and UI elements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Iosevka 是一个开源的等宽字体家族，覆盖无衬线和方衬线等多种风格。它为代码编辑器、终端和界面提供单一且可定制的字体方案，兼顾可读性与紧凑字形。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(81) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/rust-lang/rust/issues/164250' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>11 year old unsoundness bugs found in Rust borrow checker</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rust 借用检查器揭露11年前的不健全性漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50069499' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 731</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50069499' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 415</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A set of unsoundness bugs in Rust&#x27;s borrow checker, dating back 11 years, has been uncovered. The find underscores the enduring complexity of guaranteeing memory safety in a sophisticated type system and could drive targeted fixes or improved diagnostics in future releases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>揭示了 Rust 的借用检查器中存在的11年前的不健全性漏洞，凸显在复杂类型系统中长期保持内存安全的挑战。此发现可能推动针对性修复与改进诊断能力，影响未来版本的安全性保障。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
