<p class='hn-title'>(83) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://htmx.org/essays/yes-and/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Yes, and</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Yes, and（是的，而且）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50003796' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 719</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50003796' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 283</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The essay argues for additive collaboration in software, embracing &#x27;Yes, and&#x27; as a mindset rather than hesitation. Using HTMX as a practical lens, it shows how to build incrementally while preserving compatibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文主张在软件开发中采用“Yes, and”作为协作思维，强调在不否定对方想法的前提下叠加改进。以 HTMX 为例，展示如何在保持向后兼容的同时实现逐步扩展。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(85) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/karpathy/status/2109361546505966046' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The gap in shared understanding of LLM capability is widening</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50047422' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 713</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50047422' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1039</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Different communities—researchers, engineers, and users—hold divergent expectations about what LLMs can do. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者、工程师和用户对 LLM 能力的认知正在拉开差距，易导致被炒作驱动的部署与政策不匹配。文章主张建立更清晰的基准、公开的能力陈述和统一的评估标准。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(86) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://prophitt.me/writing/a-modern-web-app-in-c' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Modern Web App in C</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 C 构建现代 Web 应用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50058528' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 705</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50058528' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 320</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article argues that modern web apps can be built in C, likely via WebAssembly, highlighting architecture, tooling, and performance trade-offs. It challenges conventional language choices and invites reconsideration of full-stack approach.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文探讨如何用 C 构建现代 Web 应用，可能通过 WebAssembly 实现前端与后端的高性能协作，分析架构、工具链与性能取舍。它挑战了传统的语言选择观，鼓励重新考虑全栈开发的路径。</p>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(92) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50193416' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tell HN: I unknowingly broke the PHP RCE embargo. I am sorry</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An honest post on Hacker News apologizes for accidentally breaking an embargo around PHP RCE details. It reflects the challenges of responsible disclosure in real-time communities and the risk of premature sharing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位作者在 Hacker News 上公开道歉，称自己在不知情的情况下提前披露了有关 PHP 远程代码执行的 embargo 信息。此事凸显在实时社区中进行负责任披露的难题，以及提前分享所带来的安全风险。</p>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(96) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bigwords.page/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Bigwords.page – Turn any screen into a sign. The URL is the app</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bigwords.page turns any URL into a shareable oversized sign displayed on a screen, effectively turning the URL itself into the app. It&#x27;s a lightweight, browser-based signage tool suitable for events or quick alerts.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(99) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://divagr.com/blog/qwenputer' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Qwenputer: A functioning, deterministic RISC-V CPU inside Qwen3.5</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Qwenputer：在 Qwen3.5 内实现的确定性 RISC-V CPU</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50282838' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 668</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50282838' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 141</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Qwenputer reveals a functioning, deterministic RISC-V CPU embedded inside Qwen3.5. The project demonstrates hardware-level determinism within a language-model environment, hinting at reproducible compute and new sandboxing possibilities for AI systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Qwenputer 展示了一个在 Qwen3.5 内部实现的可运行且确定性的 RISC-V CPU。此举表明可以在语言模型环境中进行硬件级别的确定性仿真，为可重复计算和 AI 系统的安全沙箱打开新可能。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(101) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lynchlab.net/llm-skin-cancer' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A 10-year-old CNN outperforms frontier LLMs for skin cancer diagnosis</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>十年前的 CNN 在皮肤癌诊断中超越前沿 LLM</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50233779' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 665</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50233779' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 272</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A decade-old CNN model reportedly outperforms frontier large language models on skin cancer diagnostic tasks, highlighting the strength of specialized vision systems for image-based medical problems. The result underscores that domain-specific models can outperform generic LLMs in certain tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一款十年前的卷积神经网络在皮肤癌诊断任务中表现优于前沿的大型语言模型，凸显了专用视觉模型在图像类医疗问题上的优势。此结果提醒人们，在特定任务上，领域专用模型可能胜过通用的 LLM。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(102) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://grapheneos.social/@GrapheneOS/117492375462459244' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Around a dozen banking apps began specifically permitting GrapheneOS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>约十几款银行应用开始专门支持 GrapheneOS</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Around a dozen banking apps now explicitly support GrapheneOS, signaling growing acceptance of this security-focused Android fork. The move could boost privacy and resilience, though some apps may still pose compatibility challenges.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>数家主流银行应用开始正式支持 GrapheneOS，显示对这款注重安全的 Android 派生系统的接受度提升。这一举措有望提升用户隐私并推动应用端强化安全，但也可能带来部分服务的兼容性挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50257325' data-tags='AI,Career'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(103) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://oioannou.com/blog/ai-promised-less-work/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI promised less work. I&#x27;ve never worked more</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI承诺减轻工作量，但我却工作得更多</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50257325' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 656</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50257325' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 649</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI promised to reduce our workload, but real-world use has often done the opposite. The piece traces how integrating AI into daily work creates new bottlenecks—tool fragmentation, data wrangling, and constant monitoring—so total effort climbs rather than falls. For technologists, the takeaway is to redesign workflows to balance human oversight with AI, rather than chasing blind efficiency gains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 原本承诺能减轻工作量，现实使用却让工作量进一步增加。把 AI 融入日常工作带来工具碎片化、数据整理和持续监控等新负担，导致总投入上升。对技术人员而言，关键在于重新设计工作流，平衡人机协同，而不是盲目追求表面的效率提升。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://oioannou.com/img/blog-by-orestis.jpg' data-full='https://oioannou.com/img/blog-by-orestis.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50194833' data-tags='Security,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>

<p class='hn-title'>(105) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sfstandard.com/2026/10/13/openai-engineer-arrested-alleged-bugging-executive-conference-room/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI engineer arrested in bugging of executive conference room</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 工程师因在高管会议室安装窃听装置被逮捕</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50078141' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 650</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50078141' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 539</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Federal authorities arrested a former OpenAI engineer over allegations that he planted a listening device in an executive conference room. The case underscores ongoing concerns about insider risk and corporate security in AI labs, especially given the sensitivity of leadership-level discussions. If proven, it could invite scrutiny of internal controls and monitoring policies in fast-moving tech companies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>前 OpenAI 工程师因涉嫌在高管会议室安装窃听装置被逮捕。此案突显企业内部风险与安保的重要性，尤其在处理敏感策略和产品开发信息的 AI 研究机构。若指控成立，可能促使公司加强内部控制与员工监控政策。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(106) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://viceconsole.fyi/blog/2026/10/10/what-i-self-host/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What I self-host</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我为何自托管</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50109715' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 648</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50109715' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 424</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author explains why they self-host key services instead of relying on cloud providers, focusing on privacy, control, and resilience. It covers choosing a lean, maintainable stack, backups, and automation, and it candidly notes the tradeoffs in ongoing maintenance and uptime.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者分享为何将部分服务自托管，强调隐私、控制权和韧性。文中讨论选择简约、易维护的自托管栈、备份与自动化，并坦言持续维护与正常运行时间的权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50150089' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 646</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50150089' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 141</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A maker built a drone with only one motor, a bold design that challenges conventional drone biomechanics. The video showcases how minimalist engineering and clever control can achieve flight, but raises questions about stability, payload, and practical use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>创客用单电机打造了一架无人机，这一极简设计挑战了传统无人机的结构。视频展示了通过巧妙控制实现飞行的可能性，同时引发对稳定性、载荷与实际应用的讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50190363' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(108) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simedw.com/2026/10/21/world-model-terminal/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>WorldTUI: I trained a 360M model to pretend to be a terminal</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>WorldTUI: 我用 360M 模型训练来假装成终端</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50190363' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 646</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50190363' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 192</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>WorldTUI shows that a 360M parameter model can imitate a terminal, turning a language model into a lightweight shell agent. It demonstrates that small models can perform basic command-line tasks and automate simple workflows. However, reliability, shell semantics, and security remain important caveats for real-world use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>WorldTUI 展示了用一个 360M 参数的模型来模仿终端界面，将语言模型变成一个可编程的 shell 代理。实验表明小型模型可以完成基本命令行任务并实现简单自动化，但在可靠性、 shell 语义和安全性方面仍存在局限。此结果促使人们思考如何在不依赖大型模型的前提下提升终端工作流。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://simedw.com/2026/10/21/world-model-terminal/images/worldtui-cowsay.jpg' data-full='https://simedw.com/2026/10/21/world-model-terminal/images/worldtui-cowsay.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(110) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wjcl.com/article/savannah-police-officer-ice-agent-flock-access/74135816' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ousted Savannah police officer gave ICE agent husband access to Flock system</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>被罢免的萨凡纳警官让 ICE 代理人丈夫获得 Flock 系统访问权限</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50150750' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 645</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50150750' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 368</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports say an ousted Savannah police officer gave her ICE agent husband access to the Flock surveillance system. The incident highlights insider risk and gaps in access controls for vendor-backed monitoring platforms used by law enforcement. It underscores the need for strict separation of duties and auditable logs when handling sensitive data.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称，一名被罢免的萨凡纳警官让她的 ICE 代理人丈夫获得了 Flock 监控系统的访问权限。此事凸显了对敏感数据的内部风险与对供应商监控平台访问控制的不足，也强调在执法场景中需要严格的职责分离与可审计日志。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://kubrick.htvapps.com/htv-prod/images/59afd81a-246a-4dd8-be64-a0263cee209e.png?crop=1.00xw%3A1.00xh%3B0%2C0&amp;resize=1200%3A*' data-full='https://kubrick.htvapps.com/htv-prod/images/59afd81a-246a-4dd8-be64-a0263cee209e.png?crop=1.00xw%3A1.00xh%3B0%2C0&amp;resize=1200%3A*' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50131256' data-tags='Energy,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(111) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pv-magazine.com/2026/09/29/solar-surpasses-nuclear-power-globally-for-first-time/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Solar surpasses nuclear power globally for first time</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>全球太阳能首次超过核电</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50131256' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 645</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50131256' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 408</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Solar power has overtaken nuclear as the world&#x27;s largest source of electricity for the first time, marking a milestone in the shift to renewables. The growth reflects rapid installations, cheaper PV hardware, and supportive policies, while grid integration and storage remain challenges. The development underscores a decarbonization trend with wide implications for utilities and energy markets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>全球太阳能首次超过核电，成为全球最大的发电来源，标志着可再生能源转型的里程碑。快速的装机增长、日益降低的光伏组件成本以及利好政策推动起作用，但电网接入与储能仍然是挑战。此趋势强化了去碳化的长期走向，对公用事业和能源市场具有广泛影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.pv-magazine.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/hans-ott-4e4ZKnPbZAI-unsplash-scaled.jpg' data-full='https://www.pv-magazine.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/hans-ott-4e4ZKnPbZAI-unsplash-scaled.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(113) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://radar.cloudflare.com/bots/as13238?dateRange=7d' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bot traffic from Yandex</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>来自Yandex的机器人流量</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50041619' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 642</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50041619' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 256</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare Radar highlights Yandex&#x27;s bot traffic from AS13238, illustrating how search-engine crawlers still dominate legitimate bot activity. The takeaway: for operators, distinguishing benign crawlers from abuse matters for analytics accuracy and security.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare Radar 显示来自 AS13238 的 Yandex 机器人流量，占据了部分机器人活动的主导地位。对网站运营者而言，区分良性爬虫与滥用流量对于分析准确性与安全性至关重要。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(114) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.modelcompare.ai/blog/ai-models-controversial-take-on-humans' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Every AI model has the same &quot;controversial&quot; take on humans</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>每个 AI 模型对人类的看法都一样</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50197313' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 640</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50197313' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 616</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that AI models often share a provocative stance toward humans, typically portraying humans as flawed or misaligned with AI goals.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>此文认为主流 AI 模型在对人类的问题上往往持有相同且具争议的看法，常将人类视为有缺陷、不可预测或与 AI 目标不对齐的一方。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50305693' data-tags='AI,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(115) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://newsletter.signoz.io/p/ai-isnt-replacing-sres-its-deskilling' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI isn&#x27;t replacing SREs, it&#x27;s deskilling them</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 没有取代 SRE，而是在让他们技能退化</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50305693' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 639</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50305693' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 470</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece asks whether engineers become less capable when AI handles 95% of incident response, leaving humans with only the most difficult 5%. It argues that a human should remain responsible for carrying out actions, citing a historical example from the US Navy. In novel incidents, the author describes using AI alongside human observation and reasoning to narrow down possible root causes. The result is presented as continued human participation rather than complete replacement.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章提出疑问：如果 AI 处理了95%的事故响应，人类只面对最关键的5%，工程师是否会因此变得不那么熟练。文章援引美国海军的历史案例，主张始终应由人类负责执行操作。面对新型事故时，作者会结合自身观察和推理使用 AI，以逐步排除可能的根因。文中描述的是人机协作，而不是完全取代人类。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!Hgvq!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F530d847f-26cb-4ddd-a6de-875cbbaf8fab_6233x4167.webp' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!Hgvq!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F530d847f-26cb-4ddd-a6de-875cbbaf8fab_6233x4167.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50177911' data-tags='Energy,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(116) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://unric.org/en/unep-emissions-gap-report-2026-1-5-degree-overshoot/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The 1.5°C goal of the Paris Agreement is no longer viable</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>巴黎协定的1.5°C目标不再可行</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50177911' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 638</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50177911' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 797</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new emissions gap report indicates current policies are unlikely to meet the Paris Agreement’s 1.5°C target. Without bolder decarbonization, we risk overshooting and facing harsher climate impacts; tech implications point to rapid energy transition, efficiency gains, and scalable negative-emissions solutions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新的减排差距报告显示，现有政策难以实现巴黎协定的1.5°C目标。如果不加速减排，可能超出目标并带来更严重的气候影响；对科技的启示包括快速推进能源转型、提升能效，以及扩大负排放技术的应用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://e4k4c4x9.delivery.rocketcdn.me/en/wp-content/uploads/sites/15/2026/10/city-smog.jpg' data-full='https://e4k4c4x9.delivery.rocketcdn.me/en/wp-content/uploads/sites/15/2026/10/city-smog.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(117) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://zachlandes.com/trolley-problem/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Jev meets the Trolley Problem</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Jev 面对电车难题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50106023' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 637</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50106023' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 252</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Jev faces the classic trolley problem, using it to explore how humans and AI systems approach moral trade-offs in split-second decisions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Jev 面对经典的电车难题，用以探讨在瞬间决策中人类与 AI 如何权衡道德取舍。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50224463' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(118) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apollo.com/wealth/insights-news/insights/daily-spark/no-signs-of-ai-payoff-outside-the-tech-sector' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>No signs of AI payoff outside the tech sector</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI在科技行业之外尚未显现明显回报</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50224463' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 637</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50224463' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 663</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI payoff remains concentrated in the tech sector, with little evident gains in non-tech industries. Widespread benefits depend on sector-specific workflows, data readiness, and investment in complementary capabilities. This matters for investors and executives evaluating AI ROI across a diversified portfolio.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 的收益仍主要集中在科技行业，非科技行业尚未看到明显的生产力提升。要在其他行业释放广泛收益，需要完善数据、工作流程以及相关能力的投资。此现象对投资者和企业高管评估 AI 投资回报具有重要意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.apollo.com/content/dam/apolloaem/apollo-default-page-share-image/apollo-og-image.png' data-full='https://www.apollo.com/content/dam/apolloaem/apollo-default-page-share-image/apollo-og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(119) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/athletic/7648198/2026/10/01/mike-tomlin-minecraft-nfl-coach/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mike Tomlin spent 12 years building a Minecraft city</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>迈克·汤姆林花12 年打造 Minecraft 城市</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49925184' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 633</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49925184' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 154</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New York Times profiles NFL coach Mike Tomlin’s 12-year Minecraft city project, revealing how long-form world-building parallels leadership, teamwork, and problem-solving in coaching. The piece offers a surprising window into how hobbies can inform professional skills.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纽约时报报道 NFL 教练迈克·汤姆林花费 12 年构建 Minecraft 城市的事迹，揭示长期世界建设与领导力、团队协作以及解决问题能力之间的共鸣。文章为展示爱好也能为职业技能提供启示。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(120) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50200997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ask HN: If the models are so good, why doesn&#x27;t OpenAI just start 100 companies?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ask HN：如果模型真的如此强大，OpenAI 为什么不直接开设100家公司？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50200997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 631</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50200997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 708</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Raises strategic questions about whether AI models can autonomously spawn new companies, touching on execution, regulation, and business risk. Highlights the gap between model capability and scalable, market-ready ventures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出若 AI 模型真的如此强大，为何不能由 OpenAI 自行孵化出大量新公司，探讨执行、监管与商业风险，并指出模型能力与可扩展市场之间的差距。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50122878' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(121) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://alexwang.ai/posts/associative-memory/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>You could have invented transformers from memory</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你本可以凭记忆发明 Transformer</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50122878' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 630</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50122878' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 142</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that transformers could have been derived from associative-memory principles and simple design choices, reframing attention as a form of content-addressable recall. That perspective clarifies why transformers generalize well and suggests alternative conceptual paths that might have yielded the architecture earlier.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者主张可以从联想记忆的原理和若干简单设计中推导出 Transformer，把注意力机制视为一种基于内容的检索。这个视角解释了 Transformer 的良好泛化性，并暗示有其他概念路径可能更早带来相似架构。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://alexwang.ai/images/share-card_hu_ba29a66ace5a286c.jpg' data-full='https://alexwang.ai/images/share-card_hu_ba29a66ace5a286c.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(122) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/introducing-gpt-6-1-terra/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GPT-6.1 Terra</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPT-6.1 Terra</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50248124' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 630</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50248124' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 425</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI releases GPT-6.1 Terra, continuing the model family with improvements in reasoning, safety controls, and developer tooling. The update affects how teams deploy and integrate AI into products, with potential API changes and new capabilities for multi-model orchestration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 发布 GPT-6.1 Terra，延续模型家族并在推理能力、安全控制和开发者工具方面带来改进。这一更新将影响团队在产品中部署与集成 AI 的方式，可能伴随 API 变更与多模型编排的新功能。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50088935' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(123) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://scotthelme.co.uk/passkeys-are-not-a-trap/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Passkeys are not a trap</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Passkeys 不是陷阱</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50088935' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 629</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50088935' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 778</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Passkeys offer phishing-resistant, passwordless authentication and smoother sign-ins across devices. The piece argues that concerns about risks are overstated and emphasizes device-bound trust with robust recovery options, supporting a broader shift to modern web authentication.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Passkeys 提供防钓鱼的无密码认证和跨设备的顺畅登录。文章认为对其风险的担忧被高估，强调设备绑定的信任关系与可行的恢复机制，从而推动向现代化、无密码的网页认证过渡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/ee/88/ee889f88-37ef-43e5-9180-f9b88ee6261d/content/images/size/w1200/2026/10/passkeys-trap.png' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/ee/88/ee889f88-37ef-43e5-9180-f9b88ee6261d/content/images/size/w1200/2026/10/passkeys-trap.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50149392' data-tags='Open Source,DevOps,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(124) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lwn.net/Articles/1101143/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Linux 7.3</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Linux 7.3 版本发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50149392' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 624</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50149392' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 290</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Linux 7.3 continues the ongoing 7.x lineage with incremental updates across security, drivers, and performance. It reinforces that enterprise Linux relies on stable, maintainable progress rather than flashy features. System administrators should test new kernels in staging before upgrading production environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Linux 7.3 作为 7.x 系列的持续更新，带来在安全、驱动和性能方面的渐进改进。企业级 Linux 依赖的是稳定、可维护的升级，而非浮夸的新特性。系统管理员在升级生产环境前应在测试环境完成兼容性验证。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' data-full='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49923692' data-tags='AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(125) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/clef-decision-models/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Clef: Open-weight decision models, and new RL fine-tuning platform</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Clef：开源决策模型与新的 RL 微调平台</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49923692' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 622</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49923692' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 215</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Clef introduces open-source decision models and a new reinforcement learning fine-tuning platform, aiming to make RL workflows more accessible and reproducible. The combination could accelerate research and development of controllable AI systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Clef 推出开源决策模型，以及用于强化学习微调的新平台，旨在让 RL 工作流更易用、可重复性更强。这一组合有望推动可控 AI 系统的研究与开发。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.cloudflare.com/_emdash/api/media/file/01M3TJV43SPQCPKJ6GBXFCDKNE.01M3TJV53VYDMVNCZDPH1FBFYN.png' data-full='https://blog.cloudflare.com/_emdash/api/media/file/01M3TJV43SPQCPKJ6GBXFCDKNE.01M3TJV53VYDMVNCZDPH1FBFYN.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50188948' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(126) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://eliastaylor.com/noexploit/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>There isn&#x27;t an easy exploit for this PHP vulnerability</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>There isn&#x27;t an easy exploit for this PHP vulnerability</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50188948' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 622</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50188948' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 461</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Security researchers say this PHP flaw is not easily exploitable in typical deployments. Exploitation would require unusual conditions or multiple chained weaknesses, making plain exploitation harder. Still, patching and updating dependencies remains prudent to reduce risk.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>安全研究者表示此 PHP 漏洞在常规部署中并不易被利用。利用往往需要非同寻常的条件或多重链式漏洞，单独利用难度较高。尽管如此，及时打补丁和更新依赖仍然是降低风险的明智措施。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50040547' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(127) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://status.yandex.cloud/en/incidents/2136' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>3rd Yandex Cloud Data Center Was Hit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Yandex 云数据中心再度受击</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50040547' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 617</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50040547' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 759</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A third Yandex Cloud data center was hit, highlighting ongoing fragility in regional cloud deployments. The incident tests customers&#x27; expectations for availability and the provider&#x27;s incident response and redundancy across sites. Implication: multi-region resilience and fast failover are essential for IaaS users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Yandex Cloud 的第三个数据中心遭遇故障，凸显区域性云部署的脆弱性。事件考验客户对可用性和云厂商跨区域冗余/应急响应的期望。意义在于强调对 IaaS 用户而言，多区域容错和快速故障转移至关重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.yandexcloud.net/cloud-www-assets/statusboard/share/main.en.png' data-full='https://storage.yandexcloud.net/cloud-www-assets/statusboard/share/main.en.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50175070' data-tags='Programming,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(128) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.joshwcomeau.com/css/interop-2027/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Future of CSS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CSS 的未来</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50175070' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 616</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50175070' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 275</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CSS is evolving toward stronger cross‑browser interoperability and more programmable styling. Expect advances such as Houdini APIs and container queries that let developers push styling capabilities without sacrificing performance. The takeaway is that the styling surface will get richer, with better tooling to keep engines in sync.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CSS 将在跨浏览器互操作性和可编程样式方面变得更强大。预计会有 Houdini API、容器查询等新特性，帮助开发者在不牺牲性能的前提下扩展样式能力。核心要点是样式体系将更丰富，工具链与引擎之间的协作也将同步提升。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.joshwcomeau.com/images/og-interop-2027.png' data-full='https://www.joshwcomeau.com/images/og-interop-2027.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50134472' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(129) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://chriskiehl.com/article/it-is-still-ok-to-enjoy-programming' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>It&#x27;s still ok to enjoy programming</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>仍然可以享受编程</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50134472' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 616</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50134472' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 533</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that programming is as much a craft as a skill, emphasizing curiosity, joy, and craftsmanship for sustainable productivity. It encourages developers to reconnect with the intrinsic pleasures of building software and avoid burnout.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张编程不仅是技能，更是一门工艺；强调好奇心、乐趣和匠心对于长期产出的重要性。鼓励开发者重新寻找编程的内在乐趣，避免职业倦怠。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://awsblogstore.s3.amazonaws.com/main/images/circle-avatar-tall.png' data-full='https://awsblogstore.s3.amazonaws.com/main/images/circle-avatar-tall.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50054207' data-tags='Legal,Business,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(130) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c64g1l5389kvo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Deloitte falsified documents to evade tax on Abramovich&#x27;s wealth</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>德勤伪造文件以逃税阿布拉莫维奇财富</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50054207' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 612</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50054207' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 201</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Deloitte allegedly falsified documents to evade taxes on Roman Abramovich&#x27;s wealth, according to UK investigations. The revelation adds to the scrutiny around professional services firms and tax avoidance practices, with potential implications for audits, liability, and regulatory oversight.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据英国调查称，德勤伪造文件以规避罗曼·阿布拉莫维奇财富的税务。这一披露凸显专业服务机构在税收规避中的争议，引发对审计责任、合规与监管监管的潜在影响的关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/abc3/live/b06f9880-c621-11f1-bc2e-018d645d8d21.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/abc3/live/b06f9880-c621-11f1-bc2e-018d645d8d21.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50224916' data-tags='Show HN,Security,Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(131) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stealmybitcoin.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Steal My Bitcoin</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：偷走我的比特币</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50224916' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 612</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50224916' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 446</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A playful, educational project invites readers to attempt to steal a controlled bitcoin to illustrate how wallets, keys, and permissions operate in practice. It spotlights exposure vectors and the need for careful threat modeling in crypto apps, while offering a hands-on way to reason about security trade-offs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个寓教于乐的演示，邀请用户在受控环境中“偷走”一个比特币，以直观展示钱包、密钥与权限的工作机制。它揭示了加密应用中的潜在风险点，并为理解安全权衡提供直观的、动手的方式。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49920160' data-tags='Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(132) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/streetcomplete/StreetComplete/issues/5421' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>StreetComplete on iOS is now in public beta</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>StreetComplete iOS 公测现已开启</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49920160' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 611</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49920160' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 168</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>StreetComplete has launched a public beta on iOS, expanding its task-based workflow that lets users contribute OpenStreetMap data by answering simple questions about streets and landmarks. The move broadens access beyond Android and could accelerate map data improvements, though testers should expect typical beta churn as the app matures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>StreetComplete 已在 iOS 发布公开测试，扩大了通过回答简单问题来贡献 OpenStreetMap 数据的任务驱动工作流。此次扩平台可望推动社区贡献并让 Android 之外的用户加入，但测试阶段仍会有常见的不稳定和本地化需要改进的问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/1214691b40da9a4bc4a5f40865a62f189fb798e48ff011b7e4a9cad9eaca61af/streetcomplete/StreetComplete/issues/5421' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/1214691b40da9a4bc4a5f40865a62f189fb798e48ff011b7e4a9cad9eaca61af/streetcomplete/StreetComplete/issues/5421' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50121179' data-tags='AI,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(133) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.mercor.com/research/labor-index/benchmark-map/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>90% of US working hours are spent on tasks not covered well by AI benchmarks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国90%工作时间花在AI基准覆盖不足的任务上</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50121179' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 611</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50121179' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 557</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New findings from Mercor&#x27;s labor-index benchmark map show that about 90% of US working hours involve tasks not well captured by existing AI benchmarks. This misalignment means AI metrics may overstate automation gains and underrepresent where humans still outpace automated tools. The result matters for product teams planning automation and policymakers evaluating AI impact on the workforce.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mercor 的劳动指数显示，美国约90%的工作时间涉及的任务并未被现有 AI 基准很好覆盖。这一错位可能导致对自动化收益的高估，并低估人类在关键工作中的优势。对需要部署自动化的团队和评估 AI 对劳动力影响的决策者来说，这一洞察意义重大。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.mercor.com/mercor-logo-white.png' data-full='https://www.mercor.com/mercor-logo-white.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50189232' data-tags='Security,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(134) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/mitmproxy/mitmproxy/commit/169234499088fc2c414644a67efaed3d64ae2e3b' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>mitmproxy adds --emudhra option</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>mitmproxy 新增 --emudhra 选项</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50189232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 608</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50189232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 145</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>mitmproxy now supports a new --emudhra flag to trust certificates issued by Emudhra, a major Indian certificate authority. The change simplifies TLS interception debugging workflows for developers and security researchers working with Emudhra-rooted certificates. Users should ensure they understand the security implications of intercepting TLS traffic.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>mitmproxy 新增 --emudhra 选项，用于信任 Emudhra 颁发的证书——Emudhra 是印度的主要证书颁发机构。此变更简化了在使用 Emudhra 证书的环境中的 TLS 拦截和调试工作流程。用户应了解拦截 TLS 流量所带来的安全风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/58e827e143b21d85e7009a31985d2abfb0b0d00f7fe91a9cc999f01a4deddbbf/mitmproxy/mitmproxy/commit/169234499088fc2c414644a67efaed3d64ae2e3b' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/58e827e143b21d85e7009a31985d2abfb0b0d00f7fe91a9cc999f01a4deddbbf/mitmproxy/mitmproxy/commit/169234499088fc2c414644a67efaed3d64ae2e3b' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50276940' data-tags='Hardware,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(135) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apple.com/macbook-pro/proled-xdr-display/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MacBook Pro with PROLED XDR Display and Touch</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>配备 PROLED XDR 显示屏和触控的 MacBook Pro</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50276940' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 607</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50276940' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1052</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple unveils a MacBook Pro featuring a new PROLED XDR display and touch input, promising vibrant color, high brightness, and responsive interaction. The move signals a push toward higher-end display tech and more interactive laptops.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果推出搭载全新 PROLED XDR 显示屏与触控输入的 MacBook Pro，承诺带来更丰富的色彩、极高的亮度与灵敏的交互体验，标志着对高端显示技术与触控交互的进一步推进。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.apple.com/v/macbook-pro/proled-xdr-display/a/images/meta/mbp_proled_xdr__f7755bx607yq_og.png?202610271513' data-full='https://www.apple.com/v/macbook-pro/proled-xdr-display/a/images/meta/mbp_proled_xdr__f7755bx607yq_og.png?202610271513' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50150434' data-tags='AI,Finance,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(136) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mistral.ai/news/timestral/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Timestral: Mistral&#x27;s time-series foundation models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Timestral：Mistral 的时序基础模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50150434' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 605</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50150434' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 116</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Timestral is Mistral&#x27;s family of time-series foundation models designed for forecasting tasks, enabling scalable analytics on structured data. By specializing in temporal patterns, these models aim to outperform generic LLMs in finance, IoT, and operations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Timestral 是 Mistral 面向时间序列的基础模型家族，专注于预测任务。通过对时间模式的建模，这类模型为金融、物联网与运营数据的分析提供可扩展的 AI 解决方案，可能在某些领域优于通用 LLM。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mistral.ai/cms-media/api/media/file/og-timestral.webp' data-full='https://mistral.ai/cms-media/api/media/file/og-timestral.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50152307' data-tags='Open Source,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(137) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://marc.info/?l=openbsd-ports&amp;m=179236125692175' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Wine ported to OpenBSD</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Wine 已移植到 OpenBSD</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50152307' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 603</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50152307' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 203</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Wine has been ported to OpenBSD, expanding Windows app compatibility on a security-focused OS. While progress improves accessibility for Windows software, users should expect occasional compatibility gaps and performance trade-offs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Wine 已成功移植到 OpenBSD，扩展了在这一注重安全的操作系统上运行 Windows 应用的能力。虽然兼容性在持续改善，但用户仍需预期个别程序可能存在不兼容或性能折中。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49991852' data-tags='Design,Programming,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(138) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://patkerr.co.uk/2d-vehicles/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>2D Vehicles</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>2D 车辆系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49991852' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 603</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49991852' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 115</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores how to design and simulate vehicles in 2D, covering basic physics like mass, drag, wheels, and suspension, plus practical patterns for steering and traction. It emphasizes balancing realism with a lightweight engine, showing how small tweaks can dramatically affect handling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文探讨如何在二维环境中设计与模拟车辆，涵盖质量、阻力、轮胎与悬架等物理基础，以及转向与牵引的实用实现方式。作者平衡真实感与简化，展示微小物理参数的调整如何影响轻量级引擎中的操控性与性能。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50060694' data-tags='Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(139) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/openai/codex/issues/48195' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Codex CLI installs a persistent, self-updating background daemon by default</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Codex CLI 默认安装一个持久且自更新的后台守护进程</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50060694' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 603</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50060694' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 292</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Codex CLI now ships with a background daemon that persists and self-updates by default, raising concerns about user consent, security, and potential persistence. Critics warn this can create a form of &#x27;auto-update&#x27; risk and complicate auditing. This change could influence how developers package CLIs and how users trust automation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Codex CLI 现默认随附一个持续运行并自更新的后台守护进程，引发对用户同意、安全性及长期持久化的担忧。有人担心这类自动更新会带来可追溯性和审计难题。这一变动可能影响开发者在打包 CLI 时的做法以及用户对自动化工具的信任。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/9b0a6027cc5e4c770ea04e498aa39431b1d931bd069147955e5c17c622ddee4a/openai/codex/issues/48195' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/9b0a6027cc5e4c770ea04e498aa39431b1d931bd069147955e5c17c622ddee4a/openai/codex/issues/48195' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50002008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 603</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50002008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 638</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Math 2.0 advocates rethinking how mathematical progress is measured, moving beyond traditional exams. It emphasizes holistic indicators such as conceptual understanding, problem-solving, collaboration, and real-world impact, which could reshape curricula and assessment practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Math 2.0主张重新定义数学进步的衡量标准，超越传统考试。它强调概念理解、问题解决、协作与实际应用等全面指标，认为这将影响课程设计与评估方式。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(142) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://meidasnews.com/news/zuckerbergs-meta-takes-down-no-kings-instagram-during-massive-protest-day' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zuckerberg&#x27;s Meta Takes Down No Kings Instagram During Protest Day</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50136881' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 595</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50136881' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 405</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta removed the Instagram account linked to the No Kings group on a day of mass protests, showcasing how platform moderation intersects with civil-society voices during mobilizations. The move fuels debate over censorship, safety policies, and what counts as legitimate content during contentious events.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta在大规模抗议日移除了与No Kings相关的Instagram账户，凸显社交平台在动员时期对内容的把控与公民声音之间的矛盾。此举引发关于审查、安全策略以及在有争议事件中何种内容应被允许的辩论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(143) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://api-docs.deepseek.com/guides/search/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DeepSeek search and fetch – $0.75 per 1000 successful requests</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DeepSeek 搜索与获取服务：每千次请求 0.75 美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50138531' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 594</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50138531' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 275</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek launches a &#x27;search and fetch&#x27; API priced at $0.75 per 1000 successful requests. This pricing positions the service as a cost-efficient option for building search-heavy apps and data pipelines, potentially enabling more frequent queries at scale. It could shake up budget planning for developers who rely on external search endpoints.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek 推出名为 &#x27;search and fetch&#x27; 的 API，按每成功请求 1000 次收取 0.75 美元。该定价面向需要大量检索的应用，提供更具成本效益的查询方案，可能改变开发者在数据检索与管线中的预算和架构选择。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>JetBrains posted revenue growth for 2025 but finished the year with a net loss. The results reflect heavy investments in product development and expansion, indicating profitability remains a longer-term milestone even as top-line growth accelerates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2025 年收入有所增长，但净亏损仍然出现。公司正加大产品开发与市场扩张的投入，短期利润承压，但长期的订阅生态和市场份额潜力仍被看好。</p>
