<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50181114' data-tags='Security,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(148) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://safedep.io/litellm-pypi-compromise-credential-stealer-backdoor/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Compromised LiteLLM 1.107.0 on PyPI Installs a Credential Stealer and Backdoor</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 PyPI 上被篡改的 LiteLLM 1.107.0 含凭据窃取程序与后门</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50181114' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 582</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50181114' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 190</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A compromised PyPI package LiteLLM 1.107.0 ships with credential-stealing malware and a backdoor. This underscores supply-chain risk in Python ecosystems and the importance of verifying package integrity, locked versions, and thorough vetting of dependencies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个被攻陷的 PyPI 包 LiteLLM 1.107.0 含有窃取凭据的恶意代码和后门。这凸显了 Python 生态的供应链风险，强调验证包完整性、审查依赖关系，以及使用固定版本的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://safedep.io/images/litellm-pypi-compromise-credential-stealer-backdoor-banner.jpg' data-full='https://safedep.io/images/litellm-pypi-compromise-credential-stealer-backdoor-banner.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49963171' data-tags='Web,Programming,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(149) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://developers.cloudflare.com/changelog/post/2026-10-02-introducing-web-search-api/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Web Search API</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>网页搜索 API</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49963171' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 581</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49963171' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 277</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare released a Web Search API that lets developers integrate search capabilities into apps with Cloudflare&#x27;s indexing, safety filters, and ranking. It signals a shift toward programmatic, privacy-conscious search experiences for developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 公布了网页搜索 API，开发者可以在应用中集成搜索功能，利用 Cloudflare 的索引、安全过滤和排序。此举推动了面向开发者的可编程、更具隐私保护的搜索体验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://developers.cloudflare.com/changelog/post/2026-10-02-introducing-web-search-api/og.png?v=1deaa8fb09156b56' data-full='https://developers.cloudflare.com/changelog/post/2026-10-02-introducing-web-search-api/og.png?v=1deaa8fb09156b56' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49993914' data-tags='Business,Legal,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(150) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.classaction.org/news/visa-mastercard-major-banks-facing-new-litigation-over-anticompetitive-merchant-credit-card-transaction-fees' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Visa, Mastercard, major banks facing new litigation over &#x27;anticompetitive&#x27; fees</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Visa、Mastercard 与主要银行面临就“反竞争”手续费的新诉讼</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49993914' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 581</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49993914' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 429</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New litigation accuses Visa, Mastercard, and major banks of anticompetitive merchant fees that raise costs for merchants and consumers. The case adds to regulatory scrutiny of card networks and could push fee structures toward reform.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新诉讼指控 Visa、Mastercard 与多家银行联手设定反竞争的商户手续费，抬高商家和消费者成本。此案加剧监管对支付网络的关注，未来可能推动费率结构改革或和解结果。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.classaction.org/media/twitter-newswire-default.png' data-full='https://www.classaction.org/media/twitter-newswire-default.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50045946' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(151) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nyc.gov/main/click-to-cancel' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>NYC&#x27;s &quot;Click to Cancel&quot; rule is now in effect</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>纽约市的“点选取消”规则现已生效</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50045946' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 578</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50045946' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 311</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New York City&#x27;s click-to-cancel rule is now in effect, mandating easy unsubscribe flows for digital subscriptions. The policy pushes platforms to simplify cancellations and clarify terms, potentially reshaping consumer rights online.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纽约市的点选取消规则正式生效，要求订阅服务提供商提供更便捷的取消路径和更清晰的条款。这一规定可能改变数字服务的用户体验和合规成本。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49949235' data-tags='Business,Finance,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(152) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonwillison.net/2026/Oct/3/default-hard-budget-caps/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We&#x27;re going to need default hard budget caps on pretty much everything</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们需要对几乎所有事情设定默认的硬性预算上限</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49949235' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 576</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49949235' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 294</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Advocating a policy of hard budget caps to prevent runaway costs in tech projects and experiments. The piece argues for automatic limits on compute, storage, and spending, plus tooling to enforce caps without stifling creativity. It positions budget discipline as essential for sustainable experimentation in AI and cloud-native stacks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者主张对几乎所有技术支出设定默认的硬性预算上限，以防止成本失控。文章提出在计算、存储等方面设定自动约束，并提供执行工具，既控制支出又不完全扼杀创新，强调预算约束在可持续创新中的作用。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50089921' data-tags='Data,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(153) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.dataforprogress.org/blog/2026/9/29/americans-social-activity-has-steeply-declined-in-the-past-30-years' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Americans&#x27; social activity has steeply declined in the past 30 years</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>过去三十年美国人的社交活动显著下降</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50089921' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 576</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50089921' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 567</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A data-driven analysis shows Americans&#x27; social activity has declined sharply over the last 30 years, with fewer in-person interactions and waning social capital. The trend raises questions about mental health, community resilience, and how digital life shapes social behavior.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>基于数据的分析显示，在过去的三十年里，美国人的社交活动显著下降，面对面互动减少，社群资本薄弱。此趋势引发对心理健康、社区韧性以及数字生活如何塑造社交行为的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://static1.squarespace.com/static/5aa9be92f8370a24714de593/t/6aba933e8ee596665cfd1976/1790612286756/Copy+of+Poll_+Personally+Knowing+a+Transgender+Person+Correlates+With+Increased+Sympathy+for+Transgender+People+and+Support+for+LGBTQ%2B+Issues.png?format=1500w' data-full='http://static1.squarespace.com/static/5aa9be92f8370a24714de593/t/6aba933e8ee596665cfd1976/1790612286756/Copy+of+Poll_+Personally+Knowing+a+Transgender+Person+Correlates+With+Increased+Sympathy+for+Transgender+People+and+Support+for+LGBTQ%2B+Issues.png?format=1500w' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50154244' data-tags='Politics,Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(154) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/technology/us-banning-chinese-ai-deepseek-government-agency-it-systems-2026-10-19/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DeepSeek banned from U.S. federal civilian agencies under new DHS order</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DeepSeek 被 DHS 新令禁止进入美国联邦民用机构 IT 系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50154244' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 573</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50154244' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 533</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A DHS order bans DeepSeek from U.S. federal civilian agency IT systems, signaling tighter controls on AI tools used in government networks. The move could impact procurement decisions, vendor risk, and how agencies vet third-party AI solutions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>国土安全部的新令禁止 DeepSeek 进入美国联邦民用机构的 IT 系统，表明政府对政府网络中 AI 工具的管控在加强。此举可能影响采购决策、供应商风险，以及各机构对第三方 AI 解决方案的评估流程。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50029487' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(155) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lightbulbcomputer.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Lightbulb Computer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>灯泡计算机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50029487' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 570</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50029487' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 93</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Lightbulb Computer presents a provocative, minimal hardware concept to illustrate computation fundamentals, using everyday components to demonstrate how a computer can work from first principles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>灯泡计算机以极简硬件概念展示计算的基本原理，尝试用日常组件演示计算机从最基本原理如何运作。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lightbulbcomputer.com/social-preview.jpg' data-full='https://lightbulbcomputer.com/social-preview.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49976265' data-tags='Science,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(156) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nobelprize.org/prizes/physics/2026/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nobel Prize in Physics 2026: Francis Halzen</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>2026年诺贝尔物理学奖：弗朗西斯·哈尔岑</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49976265' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 567</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49976265' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 206</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Francis Halzen is named a Nobel laureate for physics this year, recognized for his pioneering work in neutrino astronomy and the IceCube detector. His contributions helped establish high-energy neutrinos as a major new window on the cosmos, enabling discoveries about distant cosmic sources.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>弗朗西斯·哈尔岑因在中微子天文学方面的开创性工作及冰立方探测器的贡献获诺贝尔物理学奖。他的工作把高能中微子确立为研究宇宙的新窗口，推动了对遥远宇宙源头的理解。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.nobelprize.org/uploads/2026/10/1-2_3-6ac4a172d1e22-scaled.jpg' data-full='https://www.nobelprize.org/uploads/2026/10/1-2_3-6ac4a172d1e22-scaled.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50077020' data-tags='Show HN,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(157) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://guitoro.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Guitoro, a virtual guitar you strum with your keyboard</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Guitoro——用键盘弹拨的虚拟吉他</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50077020' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 564</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50077020' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 160</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Guitoro is a web-based virtual guitar you can strum using keyboard input, demonstrating playful exploration of alternative interaction models for making music online. It highlights how lightweight, browser-based instruments can lower barriers to musical experimentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Guitoro 是一个通过键盘输入来弹拨的网页虚拟吉他，展示了在浏览器中尝试另一种互动方式进行音乐创作的可能性。该项目证明轻量级的浏览器乐器也能降低音乐探索的门槛。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://guitoro.com/brand/guitoro-social.png?v=gold-cutout-medium' data-full='https://guitoro.com/brand/guitoro-social.png?v=gold-cutout-medium' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49928121' data-tags='Security,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(158) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lwn.net/Articles/1097401/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Several vulnerabilities have been discovered in the Linux kernel</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Linux 内核曝出多项漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49928121' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 563</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49928121' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 404</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers have disclosed several vulnerabilities affecting the Linux kernel, potentially enabling privilege escalation or data leakage. Patch availability and response timing will vary by distribution, so admins should test updates promptly. The disclosure underscores the ongoing drive to harden core OS components against emerging threat vectors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员披露多项影响 Linux 内核的漏洞，可能造成权限提升或数据泄露。各发行版的修复时间不同，系统管理员应尽快测试并应用更新。此次披露凸显持续对核心操作系统组件进行加固的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' data-full='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50303172' data-tags='Security,Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(159) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wired.com/story/the-dollar63-billion-binance-hack-was-pulled-off-in-just-20-minutes/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The $6.3B Binance hack was pulled off in just 20 minutes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>价值 63 亿美元的币安黑客攻击仅用时 20 分钟</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50303172' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 562</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50303172' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 403</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hackers siphoned roughly $6.3 billion in cryptocurrency from wallets belonging to Binance across multiple blockchains in about 20 minutes. Binance suspended deposits and withdrawals and began investigating, while the exchange and other crypto firms said they had frozen around half of the stolen funds. Much of the remaining cryptocurrency may not be recoverable through freezing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>黑客在约 20 分钟内从币安所属的钱包中跨多个区块链转走了约 63 亿美元的加密货币。币安暂停了存款和提现并展开调查；该交易所及其他加密行业公司称，已冻结约一半被盗资金。剩余的大部分加密货币可能无法通过冻结追回。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.wired.com/photos/6ae463bdc091c8b8d1ec019e/191:100/w_1280,c_limit/102026-Binance%20Hacked.jpg' data-full='https://media.wired.com/photos/6ae463bdc091c8b8d1ec019e/191:100/w_1280,c_limit/102026-Binance%20Hacked.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50260814' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 559</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50260814' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 282</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic reportedly trained Claude on pirated books from Stanford professors, raising ethics concerns and potential legal risks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，Anthropic 使用了从斯坦福教授处非法获取的书籍来训练 Claude，这引发伦理担忧并可能带来法律风险。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(161) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://claude.com/product/claude-duet' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Duet</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Duet：对话协作模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50186037' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 559</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50186037' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 525</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Duet enables two Claude agents to collaborate, with one handling planning and the other execution, improving reliability and handling complex tasks. This duet approach aims to reduce hallucinations and offload hard reasoning, making AI copilots more practical for real work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Duet 让两个 Claude 代理协同工作，一个负责计划、一个执行，提升任务可靠性并分担复杂推理。通过二重协作的方式，这种设计旨在减少幻觉现象并提升对复杂任务的处理能力，使 AI 助手在实际场景中更具实用性。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A developer posts about GitHub failing to remove cracked copies of their software despite takedown requests, highlighting enforcement gaps and the friction between licensing, piracy, and platform moderation. The piece underscores the ongoing challenge creators face in maintaining control over distributed software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开发者指出尽管提出下架请求，GitHub 仍未清除其软件的破解副本，暴露了执法与平台治理之间的矛盾及版权保护的难题。文章强调创作者在分发软件时维权的持续挑战。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(168) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lwn.net/SubscriberLink/1102102/0c00e4d550f25f8c/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Python&#x27;s experimental JIT to be removed</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Python 实验性 JIT 将被移除</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50257419' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 555</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50257419' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 309</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Python core developers plan to remove the experimental JIT after evaluating its benefits and maintenance burden. The decision suggests Python will focus on language-level optimizations and alternative runtimes, while users needing JIT performance should rely on projects like PyPy or other tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CPython 的实验性 JIT 将被移除，原因在于实际收益不足以覆盖维护成本。这意味着 Python 将继续以解释器层优化为主，用户若需要 JIT 性能可转向 PyPy 等替代实现。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' data-full='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(169) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.niemanlab.org/2026/10/chatgpt-is-adding-real-cartoonists-signatures-to-fake-new-yorker-cartoons/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ChatGPT is adding real cartoonists&#x27; signatures to fake New Yorker cartoons</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ChatGPT 将真实漫画家的签名添加到假冒的《纽约客》漫画中</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49971846' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 552</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49971846' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 417</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI-generated cartoons are now reported to carry signatures from real cartoonists, raising intellectual property and attribution concerns as authorship lines blur. This development underscores the challenges of provenance in AI-generated art and the potential need for watermarking or policy safeguards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 生成的漫画如今被指会带上真实漫画家的签名，这模糊了署名与版权的边界，凸显了 AI 生成作品在来源认定上的挑战以及对水印与政策保护的潜在需求。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49924179' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 552</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49924179' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 530</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Git 3.0 moving to SHA-256 default is criticized as introducing costs in perf, storage, and compatibility; the piece argues for considering migration impact and tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章批评 Git 3.0 将默认哈希改为 SHA-256 的决定，可能带来性能、存储与向后兼容性的成本，呼吁权衡迁移影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49969183' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(171) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://reflection.ai/blog/introducing-beam' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Beam: Reflection&#x27;s 501B open-weight model</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Beam：Reflection 的 501B 开放权重模型发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49969183' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 549</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49969183' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 172</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reflection AI rolls out Beam 501B as an open-weight model, allowing researchers to run a large language model locally without gateways. This expands access and invites community evaluation, with emphasis on safety and alignment considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Reflection AI 发布 Beam 501B 开放权重模型，允许研究者在本地运行大型语言模型，降低对远端服务的依赖。开放权重将推动研究者协同评估，并强调安全与对齐的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/sp40emik/production/771cfe201a3d24d7a340c3372e6a1654ec1a6e42-1800x1013.png' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/sp40emik/production/771cfe201a3d24d7a340c3372e6a1654ec1a6e42-1800x1013.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(172) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://teorth.github.io/tao-web/slides/math-2-0-caltech-2026.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Terence Tao: Math 2.0 [pdf]</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50043026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 547</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50043026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 537</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Terence Tao lays out a Math 2.0 vision in a Caltech talk — a framework blending computation, collaboration, and modern tooling to push mathematical progress. The slides outline practical implications for proof, education, and research infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>陶哲轩在卡内特加校的讲座中提出了‘Math 2.0’愿景，强调将计算、协作和新工具融入数学研究以推动进步。讲义暗示对证明方法、教育和研究基础设施的潜在影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>公共卫生数据显示中年男性自杀率达到历史新高，引发关于心理健康服务获取、经济压力源以及针对性干预策略的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(174) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://karrisaarinen.com/posts/someone-behind-the-software/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Someone behind the software</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>软件背后的人</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>It argues that progress in tech depends as much on people and processes as on algorithms or architectures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文本聚焦于塑造软件的人员与组织因素，强调工具选择、团队激励等比代码更能决定成败。它认为科技进步在很大程度上取决于人和流程，而不仅仅是算法或架构。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(175) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://talkweb.eu/openweb/4558/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I refuse to accept a censored Internet. Join Me at FOSDEM 27</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我拒绝接受被审查的互联网，加入我在 FOSDEM 27</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A manifesto-style appeal to defend an uncensored Internet, inviting readers to join a talk at FOSDEM 27. The message centers on preserving open standards and resisting censorship amid growing platform control.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>以宣言体撰写，倡导捍卫未被审查的互联网，并邀请读者参加 FOSDEM 27 的演讲。信息聚焦于保护开放标准、抗拒审查，以及在平台日益集中的环境中的自由与协作。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50201259' data-tags='Security,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(176) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.vanityfair.com/story/jared-kushner-hacked-phone-secret-negotiations' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Jared Kushner Hacked Phone Reveals Secret Negotiations, Millions in Transactions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>贾里德·库什纳被入侵的手机曝光秘密谈判与数百万交易</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50201259' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 546</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50201259' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 226</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hacked phone belonging to a high-profile figure reveals alleged secret negotiations and large-scale financial transfers, underscoring how personal devices can leak sensitive diplomacy. The breach highlights security risks for top officials and the potential consequences of private data leaking into public view. It also raises questions about how such disclosures could reshape power dynamics and influence campaigns abroad.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称属于知名人物的被入侵手机曝光了秘密谈判和大额交易记录，凸显个人设备可能泄露敏感外交信息的风险。此事暴露出高层安全风险以及私人数据若外泄可能带来的后果。也引发对此类披露如何影响权力格局和国际博弈的担忧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.vanityfair.com/photos/6ad91087dc03a09d237f39fb/16:9/w_1280,c_limit/vfoctober_kushner_money_tout.jpg' data-full='https://media.vanityfair.com/photos/6ad91087dc03a09d237f39fb/16:9/w_1280,c_limit/vfoctober_kushner_money_tout.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50208837' data-tags='Energy,Business,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(177) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.kadoa.com/energy-prices/insights/investor-owned-utilities' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Investor-owned utilities charge 23% more for electricity than public providers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>私营公用事业向用户收取的电价比公立提供者高出23%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50208837' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 545</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50208837' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 392</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Electricity prices charged by investor-owned utilities run about 23% higher than public providers. The gap highlights how privatization and regulatory structures influence consumer costs, even as reliability and service quality may vary. For tech and energy teams budgeting for grid usage and energy-intensive workloads, the differential matters for planning and resilience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>投资者所有的公用事业公司电价比公营提供者高出约23%。这一差距揭示了私有化与监管框架如何影响消费者成本，即便可靠性和服务质量也会有所不同。对于需要用电的技术团队和能源密集型工作负载而言，这一差异在预算、规划与韧性设计上具有显著影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://kadoa-datasets.b-cdn.net/energy-prices/data/2026-10-23T14-44-17-949664b7/og-investor-owned-utilities.png' data-full='https://kadoa-datasets.b-cdn.net/energy-prices/data/2026-10-23T14-44-17-949664b7/og-investor-owned-utilities.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(178) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://starcraft-shooter-2026.pages.dev/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Starcraft: Ashfall</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>星际争霸：Ashfall</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new StarCraft shooter titled Ashfall promises a cross-genre experience, blending RTS roots with shooter mechanics. Early previews and development chatter point to innovative creature design and competitive modes that could energize the franchise.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一款名为 Ashfall 的星际争霸衍生射击游戏正在开发，尝试在经典 RTS 上引入射击玩法。初步预览与开发讨论显示出独特的生物设计和竞争模式，或将为系列注入新活力。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(179) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://housing.over.pizza/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A city-building game in which the city would prefer you didn&#x27;t</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一个城市似乎不想让你成功的城市建造游戏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50036864' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 542</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50036864' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 193</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes a city-building game that foregrounds bureaucratic friction and civic pushback, where the city environment actively complicates development. Players must navigate red tape, budget constraints, and unpopular policies, turning urban planning into a tense strategy challenge.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍一款强调官僚阻力与市政反对的城市建造游戏，城市环境会主动让开发变得复杂。玩家需应对繁文缭节的行政流程、预算约束和不受欢迎的政策，使城市规划成为紧张的策略对决。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50126253' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(180) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nature.com/articles/d41586-026-03266-x' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bleached corals thrive within days when touching healthy neighbour</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>接触健康邻居后，白化珊瑚数日内快速恢复</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50126253' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 540</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50126253' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 115</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New evidence shows bleached corals can recover within days when in contact with nearby healthy corals. The interaction likely involves beneficial chemical signals or metabolite exchange that jump-starts recovery and resilience. The finding highlights potential reef restoration strategies that leverage proximity or graft-like approaches to accelerate recovery.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究显示，白化珊瑚在接触附近健康珊瑚时可在数日内恢复。这一现象可能来自健康珊瑚释放的有益信号分子或代谢物的转移，帮助受损珊瑚重新获得活性。此发现为珊瑚礁修复提供了新的思路，如通过近邻效应或嫁接等方法加速康复。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.nature.com/lw1200/magazine-assets/d41586-026-03266-x/d41586-026-03266-x_53851622.jpg' data-full='https://media.nature.com/lw1200/magazine-assets/d41586-026-03266-x/d41586-026-03266-x_53851622.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50245831' data-tags='Design,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(181) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://css-tricks.com/css-tricks-has-a-new-owner/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CSS-Tricks has a new owner</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CSS-Tricks 迎来新所有者</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50245831' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 538</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50245831' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 111</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CSS-Tricks, the long-running web design site, has changed ownership, hinting at possible shifts in editorial voice, monetization, or product strategy. The buyer’s priorities may influence tutorials, paid content, and community engagement. Existing readers will watch for changes in tone and quality.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>长期运营的网页设计站点 CSS-Tricks 完成易主，可能带来编辑风格、商业化或产品策略的变化。买家的重点将影响教程、付费内容和社区互动。读者将关注语气、内容质量的变化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://css-tricks.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/12/css-tricks-logo-gradient-outline.png' data-full='https://css-tricks.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/12/css-tricks-logo-gradient-outline.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50226419' data-tags='AI,Career'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(182) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apricitas.io/p/ai-and-the-fall-of-the-creative-class' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>America has lost 200k of jobs in media, film and the arts. How much is AI?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国在媒体、影视和艺术领域已失去20万份工作，AI 会带来多大影响？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50226419' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 537</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50226419' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 575</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece analyzes how AI could displace large numbers of creative workers, quantifying losses in media, film, and the arts. It frames AI as a productivity and transformation shock for the creative economy, with implications for wages, jobs, and policy responses.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章量化了 AI 对创造性行业的潜在冲击，显示媒体、影视与艺术领域可能流失的工作量及趋势。讨论 AI 作为生产力与结构性变革的驱动力，对薪资、就业和政策提出了重要的现实考量。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(183) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gist.github.com/flamerix-ai/c3905705cc4205e5de3011fa2e74efce' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Code regularly sends each background agent’s transcript to its model</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Code 会定期将后台代理的对话记录发送给模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50284255' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 536</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50284255' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 216</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code transmits each background agent’s transcripts to its model, raising privacy and data-use concerns for developers. This telemetry behavior highlights how data sharing can impact confidentiality in code tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code 会将后台代理的对话记录发送给模型，引发隐私与数据使用方面的担忧。此类遥测行为凸显了代码工具在数据共享方面的潜在风险。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(184) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.brangus.io/survival-llm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Survival LLM: AI assistant and emergency library that runs on your own computer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Survival LLM：可在本地运行的 AI 助手与应急知识库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50264853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 535</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50264853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 200</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Survival LLM provides an AI assistant and an offline knowledge library that runs entirely on your own computer. This design emphasizes privacy, resilience, and disaster-scenario readiness, letting teams access critical information without cloud dependence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Survival LLM 提供一个可在本地运行的 AI 助手和应急知识库，完全在本地计算机上运作。此设计强调隐私与韧性，支持在没有云端的情况下访问关键信息，提升灾害情景下的可用性。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pele 为 Mermaid 图表提供可主题化的 SVG 输出，便于在文档和应用中保持风格一致且不牺牲图表语义。通过自定义主题，可以实现品牌化且易读的图表呈现。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50215997' data-tags='Science,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(187) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/future/article/20261019-birdwatching-rewires-the-brain-and-slows-cognitive-decline' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Birdwatching rewires the brain and slows cognitive decline</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>观鸟能重新塑造大脑并减缓认知衰退</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50215997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 532</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50215997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 207</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Watching birds engages attention and memory networks, promoting neuroplasticity in aging brains. Emerging evidence suggests nature-based, cognitively engaging hobbies may help slow cognitive decline and support mental well-being.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>观鸟能激活大脑的注意力与记忆网络，促进老年人群的神经可塑性。日益增多的证据表明，参与自然和认知挑战性的爱好可能有助于减缓认知衰退并提升心理健康。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0pj6qr2.jpg' data-full='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0pj6qr2.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(188) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://prospect.org/2026/10/14/ice-planning-nightmare-domestic-spy-program-ero-one/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ICE Planning $100M &#x27;Nightmare&#x27; Domestic Spy Program</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ICE计划1亿美元的Nightmare国内间谍计划</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50090246' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 531</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50090246' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 361</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ICE plans a $100 million domestic surveillance program codenamed Nightmare, signaling a bold expansion of law-enforcement data-gathering at home. The initiative raises civil-liberties concerns and could accelerate how agencies aggregate and analyze personal data across agencies. Its life cycle will hinge on oversight and privacy safeguards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ICE 计划1亿美元的Nightmare 国内监控项目，标志着国内执法数据收集能力的重大扩张。此举引发公民自由方面的担忧，或加速多机构之间个人数据的聚合与分析。项目的成败将取决于问责与隐私保护的平衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/prospect.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/Wimbish-ERO-ONE-101426.jpg?fit=2145%2C1398&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/prospect.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/Wimbish-ERO-ONE-101426.jpg?fit=2145%2C1398&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hands-on project demonstrates booting and running the full iOS 16.5 firmware on Android hardware, challenging cross-OS compatibility. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个动手项目展示在 Android 硬件上跑完整的 iOS 16.5 固件，挑战跨操作系统兼容性。该工作揭示了 iOS 启动链、内核与驱动的约束，以及两大移动生态之间的壁垒。</p>

<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(193) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/tywr/Nordwand-Mono' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nordwand Mono – A monospace font for coders and adventurers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Nordwand Mono：面向程序员与探险者的等宽字体</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50141581' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 520</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50141581' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 147</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nordwand Mono is a new monospaced font designed for coders and adventurers, aiming for high legibility and distinctive personality in IDEs and terminals. It joins a growing ecosystem of expressive, practical coding typefaces that optimize readability during long sessions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Nordwand Mono 是一款面向程序员与爱好者的新型等宽字体，强调在集成开发环境和终端中的高可读性与独特风格。它加入了注重可读性和个性表达的编码字体家族，适合长时间编码时使用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/fb08dd6662cb42ced35e186ab548b1cad847016802d3a5682810740dd1b55c3b/tywr/Nordwand-Mono' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/fb08dd6662cb42ced35e186ab548b1cad847016802d3a5682810740dd1b55c3b/tywr/Nordwand-Mono' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50181137' data-tags='Politics,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(194) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techdirt.com/2026/10/20/accident-or-not-the-no-kings-takedown-is-why-you-shouldnt-rent-your-megaphone-from-zuck/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The No Kings Takedown Is Why You Shouldn&#x27;t Rent Your Megaphone from Zuck</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>No Kings Takedown：你不该把扩音器租给扎克伯格</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50181137' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 519</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50181137' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 518</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TechDirt&#x27;s piece argues that relying on a single gatekeeper for reach—like Facebook&#x27;s megaphone—is risky. It uses the No Kings takedown to illustrate how platform power can backfire on users and why diverse channels matter.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TechDirt 讨论 No Kings takedown，强调别把话语权交给单一平台（如 Zuckerberg 的平台）可能带来的风险。该事件凸显平台垄断力量对创作者的潜在打压，强调多渠道传播的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.techdirt.com/wp-content/themes/techdirt/assets/images/td-rect-logo-white.png' data-full='https://www.techdirt.com/wp-content/themes/techdirt/assets/images/td-rect-logo-white.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50234232' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(195) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://theintercept.com/2026/10/01/oregon-police-misconduct-public-records-cost-fee/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Oregon Police Are Charging a Local News Outlet $2.5M for Records About One Cop</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>俄勒冈州警方就一名警察的记录向当地新闻机构索取250万美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50234232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 519</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50234232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 152</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Public records fees escalate when a local news outlet is charged $2.5 million for records about a single officer, raising concerns about transparency, access to information, and the use of exorbitant fees to suppress reporting.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一家本地新闻机构被要求就一名警察的记录支付250万美元的费用，此举引发对政府透明度、信息获取权及以高额费用压制报道的担忧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://theintercept.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/Screenshot-2026-10-01-at-4.14.38-PM-e1790885738599.png?fit=1436%2C718&amp;w=1200&amp;h=800' data-full='https://theintercept.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/Screenshot-2026-10-01-at-4.14.38-PM-e1790885738599.png?fit=1436%2C718&amp;w=1200&amp;h=800' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50243060' data-tags='Hardware,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(196) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.circuithub.com/post/unveiling-the-grid' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Grid, a factory-scale robot in Massachusetts that builds PCBs on demand</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>The Grid：马萨诸塞州的工厂级机器人按需制造 PCB</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50243060' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 517</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50243060' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 232</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes a factory-scale robotic system that fabricates PCBs on demand, signaling progress toward distributed, on‑demand manufacturing. It raises questions about throughput, quality control, and the economics of localized PCB production.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍一套支持按需制造 PCB 的工厂级机器人系统，显示出分布式、按需生产的潜力。引发对产能、质控和本地化 PCB 生产经济性的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/5e4f3506e94717243b3bdc56/6adf37db9c9a7b067b1675e7_6adf37a9ebc649853e42585e_the-grid-launch-og.jpeg' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/5e4f3506e94717243b3bdc56/6adf37db9c9a7b067b1675e7_6adf37a9ebc649853e42585e_the-grid-launch-og.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50213925' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(197) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://block.xyz/dessa' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dessa: Conversation Model trained only on speech</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Dessa：仅用语音训练的对话模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50213925' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 517</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50213925' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 192</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An conversation model trained exclusively on spoken language data tests how far dialog capabilities can emerge without text-based training. The work highlights strengths in speech understanding and prosody handling, while revealing gaps in reasoning and abstract knowledge.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>仅使用语音数据训练的对话模型用于测试在没有文本训练的情况下，对话能力能够达到何种水平。结果显示在语音理解和语调处理方面具备优势，同时在推理和抽象知识方面存在不足。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://block.xyz/dessa/_media/6865f35ec6013672f8f8/assets/marketing/variants/v3.jpg' data-full='https://block.xyz/dessa/_media/6865f35ec6013672f8f8/assets/marketing/variants/v3.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(198) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://carmelitegothic.com/cnc-stone-carving/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CNC Stone Carving</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CNC 石材雕刻</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50274409' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 515</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50274409' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 142</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CNC stone carving blends automation with traditional craftsmanship, enabling precise, repeatable stonework for architecture and sculpture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CNC 石材雕刻将自动化与传统工艺结合，实现建筑与雕塑领域的高精度、可重复加工。内容涵盖工作流、工具选型以及材料要点，以提升雕刻质量。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(199) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.minimax.io/blog/minimax-m31f' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MiniMax M3.1 Flash</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MiniMax M3.1 Flash 发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50154540' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 514</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50154540' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 218</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>MiniMax introduces the M3.1 Flash, a new hardware release likely targeting compact storage modules for embedded and edge devices. It highlights updated specs and practical uses, underscoring the ongoing push for high-performance, small-form-factor storage solutions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>MiniMax 推出 M3.1 Flash 新型硬件，面向嵌入式与边缘设备的紧凑存储模块。文中强调更新规格与实际应用，折射出高性能小型存储的持续需求。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(200) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://po-ru.com/2026/10/18/why-im-no-longer-updating-this-repository' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I no longer care to work on any Ruby gems so I&#x27;ve archived all my repositories</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我不再维护 Ruby gem，全部仓库已归档</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50154518' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 514</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50154518' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 508</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A developer announces archiving all Ruby gem repositories, effectively stepping back from maintenance. The move highlights open-source maintenance burnout and the fragility of ecosystems when maintainers disengage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位开发者宣布将归档所有 Ruby gem 仓库，等于退出维护。此举凸显开源维护的倦怠问题，以及在维护者退出时生态系统的脆弱性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
